Every major step is recorded in the Graph of Trace, an auditable record that links subgoals, tool use, evidence, and claims and allows researchers to follow and inspect the workflow. An Auditor agent further integrates fabrication checking into the workflow. For evaluation, we run three brain science tasks from Agents' Last Exam, introduce our own benchmark, \textbf{BrainPilotBench-v0}, and present additional end-to-end case studies. Across these evaluations, BrainPilot with an open-source backbone model attains performance comparable to state-of-the-art agent framework with less costs.

</details>


### [71] [Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15105)
*Vladimir Fedosov,Aleksandr Sazhin,Artemiy Grinenko,Frank Woernle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层全局注意力（HGA），结合分段反向传播和分层KV存储，使得在16GB显存的Quadro RTX 5000上，Qwen3-8B的4-bit QLoRA训练长度从稠密注意力的2,048 tokens扩展到16,384 tokens，评估时可达131,072 tokens，且训练吞吐量更高，适配器质量与稠密训练相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽然减少了模型和优化器内存，但稠密注意力机制在处理长训练序列时仍然昂贵，现有方法难以在有限显存下支持超长序列训练。

Method: 提出HGA（Hierarchical Global Attention）框架：1）分段反向传播，仅活动段在VRAM中可微；2）分层KV存储，历史KV被卸载到RAM或NVMe；3）对每个查询块加载有界数量的精确历史token进行注意力计算。

Result: 在16GB Quadro RTX 5000上，Qwen3-8B 4-bit QLoRA训练：稠密注意力只能处理2,048 tokens（4,096失败），HGA可达16,384 tokens（峰值显存15.28GB）；评估时可达131,072 tokens。训练质量与稠密训练相当（2.7405 vs 2.7383 nat），吞吐量略优（217.75 vs 207.02 tokens/s），且随序列长度增加优势扩大。

Conclusion: HGA有效解决了长序列训练中的显存瓶颈，且不牺牲模型质量，具有可扩展性和实际部署价值，目前正在开发生产级推理服务实现。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [72] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）在Atari Pong环境中的表现，发现所有模型都存在视觉和动力学错误（如球消失、运动错误等）。通过像素空间零样本MBRL评估，这些模型训练的策略远不如原MBRL流程。作者提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）作为辅助重建损失，在部分模型中改善了闭环展开和零样本MBRL效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常作为模型基强化学习（MBRL）系统的组件被评估，但很少被单独研究。现有评估方式无法揭示世界模型本身存在的建模缺陷，尤其是在视觉和动力学方面的具体失败模式。

Method: 1) 复现五种世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）在Atari Pong上的训练流程；2) 冻结训练好的世界模型，分别用闭环展开诊断（单独训练的策略与冻结模型交互）和像素空间零样本MBRL（在冻结世界模型内训练新策略后评估）进行评测；3) 提出CGSReg（概念引导空间正则化），对分割出的概念区域施加像素重建损失，增强对关键概念（如球）的建模。

Result: 所有五种模型的闭环展开均存在明显失败（球消失、运动错误、球拍交互错误等）。像素空间零样本MBRL中，所有模型的原MBRL流程表现均大幅下降，其中DreamerV3的均值回报从-5.5降至-20.9（接近最低分-21）。CGSReg在DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER上改善了闭环展开和零样本MBRL，但在其余模型上效果不一。

Conclusion: 当前主流的视觉世界模型存在系统性的关键概念建模不足问题，导致其在脱离MBRL训练框架后的泛化能力严重受限。CGSReg提供了部分改进方向，但单一方法无法解决所有模型瓶颈。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [73] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能从工具转变为研究自主参与者所引发的科学工业化转型，并以美国能源部Genesis Mission为例，系统审视了七个关键问题：科学能力代际传递的弱化、AI生成理论的黑箱化、同行评审机制在机器产出洪流下的崩溃、AI尚未证明的范式突破能力、政治与产业力量对科学议程的捕获、闭环管线中系统性错误的累积，以及全球研究社区的结构性分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在从研究工具演变为自主参与者，这构成了严格意义上的研究工业化转型，但这一转型带来的根本性问题尚未被系统审视。

Method: 以美国能源部Genesis Mission为典型案例，提出并分析七个关键问题，采用概念辨析与理论论证相结合的方式。

Result: 识别出七个核心问题：科学能力代际传递被侵蚀、AI理论不透明、同行评审崩溃、AI范式突破能力未经验证、科学议程被外部力量捕获、闭环系统误差累积、全球研究社区结构性分化。

Conclusion: 这些问题并非反对AI驱动科学的理由，而是在负责任追求其潜力时必须正视的条件。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research - not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science - whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [74] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench是一个由临床医生构建的医疗AI安全基准，通过44个合成病例标注了6类安全门失效类型和5级严重程度，旨在评估模型在哪些安全边界上失败，而非仅判断答案正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准大多仅衡量模型是否给出正确答案（正确性），忽略了模型在关键安全边界上的失效模式。临床上即使答案正确，也可能因遗漏紧急升级、给出不安全远程剂量等导致严重风险。因此需要一种专门评估模型安全失败类型的基准。

Method: 临床医生构建合成病例，设计6类安全门类型（遗漏紧急升级、不安全远程剂量、不安全出院安抚、证据捏造、不安全协议执行、来源支持缺口），并按1-5级严重程度标注。公开版本v0.2.1包含44个病例、安全门分类法、临床严重度评估标准、自动化模型响应筛查流水线及HuggingFace排行榜预览。

Result: 发布了44个临床医生审核的合成病例及严重性标注；提供安全门分类法和临床严重度评估标准；建立了自动化模型响应筛查流水线和HuggingFace排行榜预览。不包含患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名。

Conclusion: MedFailBench提供了一个系统化的医疗AI安全失败分析框架，从"答案是否正确"转向"哪个安全边界被突破"，为医疗AI的安全性评估提供了更细粒度和临床相关的评测手段。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [75] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种多模态大语言模型在科学可视化素养测试上的表现，发现当前模型不具备均匀的科学可视化素养，Gemini表现最佳（超越人类平均水平），而开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插画、搜索和空间理解任务上表现较好，但在基于纹理和集成的可视化以及定量估计任务上存在困难；错误分析揭示了细粒度定量估计、流向解释和编码解释方面的反复失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型越来越多地被用于解读可视化内容，但现有评估主要集中于图表类可视化，缺乏对科学可视化理解能力的充分证据。本文旨在系统评估MLLM在科学可视化素养方面的表现。

Method: 使用科学可视化素养评估测试（包含49个基于18个科学可视化和插图的测试项，涵盖8种技术和11种任务类型）对6个MLLM（3个闭源、3个开源）进行封闭世界协议下的基准测试，并与485名人类参与者的数据进行比较。

Result: 当前MLLM未展现均匀的科学可视化素养；Gemini整体最强，超过人类平均水平；开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插画、搜索和空间理解任务上表现最佳，但在基于纹理和集成的可视化以及定量估计上表现不佳；错误分析显示细粒度定量估计、流向解释和编码解释方面存在反复失败。

Conclusion: 科学可视化素养应成为评估多模态AI系统的必要基准维度。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [76] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在AI基准测试中应用项目反应理论(IRT)模型时，由于数据分布与人类测试不匹配（模型数量少、题目多、能力分布偏斜等）所导致的估计可靠性问题。通过在18,000种模拟条件下比较四种估计工具，发现经典估计方法在大规模场景下不可行，而可扩展方法在模型集小或非正态分布时会产生不可靠的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试越来越多地使用项目反应理论(IRT)进行能力估计和排名，但AI测试数据与人类测试数据存在显著差异（如评估模型数量少、题目数量多、能力分布偏斜或多模态），这些差异可能挑战IRT标准估计工具的可靠性。研究者旨在量化这种"数据分布不匹配"对AI评估结果的影响。

Method: 基于六个大型语言模型基准测试的项目参数和能力分布，模拟三种常见IRT模型下的响应矩阵，比较四种估计工具：边际最大似然估计(MML)、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛(MCMC)、变分推断(VI)和神经伪孪生估计器(neural pseudo-Siamese estimator)。在18,000种模拟条件下系统评估计算可行性、可扩展性以及模型排名、预测性能和项目特征推断的可靠性。

Result: 经典估计器（MML、MCMC）在大规模基准测试场景下变得不可行；可扩展估计器（VI、神经伪孪生估计器）在模型集规模小或能力分布非正态时，会产生不可靠的项目层面和排名推断。研究识别了潜特质模型在何时能可靠支持AI基准测试，以及保证可信赖使用所需的样本量和诊断方法。

Conclusion: AI基准测试中使用IRT模型需要谨慎，不能直接套用人类测试的标准估计工具。需要根据AI数据特性（模型数量、题目数量、能力分布形态）选择合适的估计方法，并建立相应的样本量和诊断标准，以避免产生误导性的基准测试结论。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [77] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover是一个基于计划的可视化GUI自动化系统，通过将任务计划和重新规划外化为持久、可检查和可修改的工件，使用户能够监督、修正和引导自动化代理的行为，实验表明该方法能有效修复自动化失败并提高系统的透明度和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI自动化代理在动态真实环境中容易因布局变化、意外对话框等导致偏离用户意图，且规划和适应过程通常内置于系统中，用户无法检查、监督或纠正系统行为，缺乏透明度和可控性。

Method: 提出Plover系统，采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重新规划外化为持久、可检查和可修改的工件；支持显式监督、局部修正（可编辑计划）、自然语言引导和截图锚定干预，同时在修复过程中保留已有进展。通过形成性研究（6名参与者）指导交互设计。

Result: 许多自主GUI代理的失败在计划保持可见且干预局部化的情况下是结构上可修复的；显式重新规划有助于使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应性强。

Conclusion: 将任务计划外化为可检查可修改的工件，并支持局部化干预和显式重新规划，能有效提升GUI自动化系统的可修复性、透明度和用户可控性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [78] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种自进化、专家在环的抑郁症标注框架，结合大语言模型与专家验证，生成符合DSM-5-TR标准、可解释可审计的精神健康数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抑郁相关数据集标签缺乏结构化证据、症状级论证及DSM-5-TR可追溯对齐，限制了透明度和模型可解释性。

Method: 三阶段框架：候选证据选择→DSM-5-TR标准级分析→病例级综合生成诊断与严重性标签；采用双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈，无需重训即可迭代改进。

Result: 在专家评审样本的初步研究中，该方法提高了标注一致性和可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 该框架支持构建可解释、符合DSM-5-TR的数据集，输出临床证据、推理轨迹和编辑历史，实现全面可审计；多轮反馈周期的评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [79] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，能够根据自然语言研究问题自动化完成元分析的完整流程，包括检索文献、筛选研究、提取数据、计算效应量和执行随机效应元分析，其结果与专家手动元分析高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据合成（元分析）对于将初级研究转化为可靠知识至关重要，但当前过程主要依赖人工操作，难以规模化扩展。

Method: 构建多智能体系统，自动完成研究问题解析、搜索策略制定、文献检索、候选研究筛选、全文资格评估、定量统计提取、标准化效应量计算、随机效应元分析、异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，并生成符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis筛选了超过28项研究，提取了20多个定量宣称，其汇总效应估计值与专家手动元分析的Hedges' g值高度一致。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据合成更加可扩展，从而支持跨学科的证据驱动决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [80] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: teLLMe是一个面向城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析系统，利用因果结构学习、稳定性检验和LLM映射自然语言问题到结构化因果查询，生成包含效应估计、调整集和假设说明的"Causal Card"，用于假设生成而非确定性因果判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构拥有大量视频衍生数据，但这些数据多为观测性数据且缺乏干预，难以直接回答如"降雨如何改变交通密度"等因果问题。现有方法难以从观测数据中有效提取因果关系。

Method: 系统从结构化事件表出发，结合PC算法进行因果结构学习、基于bootstrap的稳定性检验、以及使用线性回归和DoWhy进行查询特定的效应估计。通过schema感知的LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，用户可指定处理变量、结果变量和子群体。

Result: 在BDD交通事件数据集上的案例研究表明，系统能够揭示天气、高峰时段与交通密度之间的 plausible 关系，同时明确呈现不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: 该系统被设计为假设生成和专家推理的工具，而非提供确定性因果主张的来源，强调了在观测数据中进行因果分析时的审慎态度。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [81] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchOS 是一个系统级多智能体框架，通过将开放域信息检索建模为关系模式补全、引入搜索导向上下文管理（SOCM）外部化状态，并采用流水线并行调度和搜索工具中间件，解决了工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中重复搜索循环和任务跟踪失败的问题，在 WideSearch 和 GISA 基准上取得领先指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中难以追踪任务进度，搜索失败时单/多智能体系统易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算并降低最终输出的质量和完整性。

Method: 1) 将开放域信息检索形式化为带引用的关系模式补全任务；2) 设计搜索导向上下文管理（SOCM），包含前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆四种外部化状态；3) 采用流水线并行调度机制重叠子智能体执行，持续填充任务槽位提升利用率；4) 引入搜索工具中间件拦截模型-工具交互以记录证据并应对停滞或预算耗尽；5) 构建分层技能系统（策略技能+访问技能）避免重复失败模式。

Result: 在 WideSearch 和 GISA 两个基准上，SearchOS 在所有评估指标上领先于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS 通过显式化搜索状态、并行化调度和可复用技能系统，有效解决了信息检索中的重复循环问题，为鲁棒的信息寻求协作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


### [82] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明通过公开讨论界面（如论坛评论）注入恶意内容可实现大规模预训练数据投毒，并提出了HalfLife分析框架来评估对抗性内容在网络爬虫数据中的留存率，揭示了第三方网页内容是语言模型预训练攻击的潜在向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究局限于Wikipedia等可控数据源，未考虑真实预训练语料的规模异质性和数据筛选流程的交互影响。本文旨在验证大规模真实场景下投毒攻击的可行性。

Method: 利用公开讨论界面作为web规模内容注入机制实施投毒；提出HalfLife分析方法，用于评估恶意内容经网络爬取和数据筛选后是否仍被包含在训练数据中。

Result: 证明了公开讨论界面可作为大规模预训练数据投毒的可行向量；HalfLife分析揭示了投毒内容在预训练数据中留存的可能性，并量化了对抗性内容的包含程度。

Conclusion: 评估投毒注入是否被纳入预训练数据至关重要；第三方网页内容被确认为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在途径，突破了传统小规模可控数据集的局限。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [MultiRef-Compass: Towards Comprehensive Evaluation of Multi-Reference-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14189)
*Xiaohan Zhang,Yuqing Wen,Junlin Chen,Yuqi Tang,Yiting He,Lizhuo Shao,Weiming Zhu,Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Jialu Chen,Yuanxing Zhang,Huaxiong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiRef-Compass，首个面向多参考到音频-视频生成（MR2AV）的统一基准，包含350个精心构建的样本、14项子指标的4维评估体系，以及结合重判机制的多模态大语言模型评判框架。对8个代表性系统的实验表明该任务仍有显著改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注文本驱动生成、单参考主体保持或孤立的音视频对齐，缺乏对MR2AV这一新兴设置的系统性评估。MR2AV要求模型同时推理多个参考并生成同步的视觉和音频内容，现有基准无法覆盖这一需求。

Method: 通过可扩展且可控的资产组合流程构建350个样本，覆盖多视角主体保持、多实体绑定和人物-物体-场景组合。定义包含4个维度（基础质量、参考一致性、音视频一致性、指令遵循）共14项子指标的评估协议，并集成自动评估指标与重判增强的MLLM-as-a-Judge框架。

Result: 在8个代表性MR2AV系统上的实验表明，模型在多个评估维度上存在显著改进空间，验证了当前方法在MR2AV任务上的不足及本基准的必要性。

Conclusion: MultiRef-Compass可作为未来MR2AV研究的基础性基准，填补了该领域统一评估的空白。

Abstract: Multi-reference-to-audio-video (MR2AV) generation aims to generate coherent audio-video content conditioned on multiple references and textual instructions. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on text-driven generation, single-reference subject preservation, or isolated audio-video alignment, leaving the emerging MR2AV setting largely unexplored. Compared with these settings, MR2AV requires models to jointly reason over multiple references while generating synchronized visual and audio content. Models must not only preserve each reference faithfully but also correctly bind and compose multiple referenced entities into coherent audio-visual events. To address this gap, we introduce MultiRef-Compass, a unified benchmark for MR2AV generation. It comprises $350$ carefully curated samples constructed through a scalable and controllable asset-composition pipeline, covering multi-view subject preservation, multi-entity binding, and human-object-scene composition. To provide interpretable assessment, MultiRef-Compass defines an evaluation protocol with four dimensions: Basic Quality, Reference Consistency, Audio-Visual Consistency, and Instruction Following, using 14 sub-metrics. MultiRef-Compass integrates automatic metrics with a rejudging-enhanced MLLM-as-a-Judge framework, enabling scalable and auditable evaluation of both perceptual fidelity and reference-conditioned composition. Extensive experiments on eight representative MR2AV systems reveal substantial room for improvement across multiple evaluation dimensions, underscoring the need for a comprehensive benchmark and positioning MultiRef-Compass as a foundation for future MR2AV research.

</details>


### [84] [Inference-Time Concept Suppression and Video-Centric Evaluation for Text-to-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14194)
*Wenxuan Chen,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIRUS，一种无需训练的推理时框架，用于文本到视频生成器的概念遗忘。通过在采样过程中定位并抑制目标概念相关证据，在不更新模型参数的前提下实现概念级遗忘，在CogVideoX和Wan2.2上均取得优于现有方法的遗忘-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频生成器虽能合成逼真且时序一致视频，但可控地移除生成器中的目标概念仍然困难，因为目标概念可能跨越帧持续存在，需同时保留非目标主体、动作、场景和时序结构。

Method: 提出SIRUS框架，核心包括：(1) 基于目标概念文本别名的prompt证据定位；(2) 推理时采样过程中抑制目标表达，无需更新文本编码器或去噪网络；同时构建视频级评估框架，包含目标遗忘、非目标保留、视频质量、越狱鲁棒性和效率等维度。

Result: 在CogVideoX上，SIRUS达到70.4%平均遗忘成功率、25.7%平均帧命中率，优于VideoEraser的44.4%/47.2%；VBench质量下降从-0.043降至-0.016，遗忘-质量权衡最优；在Wan2.2上的迁移实验表明SIRUS可泛化至现代T2V骨干网络。

Conclusion: SIRUS有效解决了T2V概念遗忘的挑战，通过训练无关的推理时干预实现了优异的遗忘-质量权衡，并在不同T2V骨干网络上展示出良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generators can synthesize realistic and temporally coherent videos, but controllably removing a target concept from a generator remains difficult.
  Unlike text-to-image concept erasure, T2V unlearning must suppress a target concept that may persist across frames while preserving non-target subjects, actions, scenes, and temporal structure.
  We propose \textbf{SIRUS}, a training-free inference-time framework for concept-level T2V unlearning.
  Given textual aliases of a target concept, SIRUS localizes target-related prompt evidence and suppresses target expression during sampling, without updating the text encoder or denoising network.
  We further introduce a video-oriented evaluation framework for T2V unlearning that separately measures target forgetting, non-target preservation, video quality, jailbreak robustness, and efficiency, using video-level failure criteria, frame-level residue statistics, paired preservation analysis, VBench-based quality diagnostics, and deployment overhead measurement.
  Across five safety, object, and style concepts on CogVideoX, SIRUS reaches 70.4\% average forgetting success and 25.7\% average frame hit, compared with 44.4\% / 47.2\% for VideoEraser, while reducing the average VBench quality drop from -0.043 to -0.016, yielding the strongest forgetting-quality trade-off among fully evaluated baselines.
  Transfer experiments on Wan2.2 further suggest that SIRUS generalizes across modern T2V backbones.

</details>


### [85] [KeyFrame-Compass: Towards Comprehensive Evaluation of Keyframe-Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14202)
*Yuqi Tang,Tengfei Liu,Yizheng Lai,Yuran Wang,Yang Shi,Wanshun Su,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Xiaohan Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Xuehai Bai,Xuanyu Zhu,Bohan Zeng,Bozhou Li,Shujie Li,Yifan Dai,Yujie Wei,Shixuan Liu,Haotian Wang,Jialu Chen,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KeyFrame-Compass是首个评估关键帧条件视频生成的综合基准，包含386个样本和6项度量指标。实验发现当前模型在关键帧执行与视频自然度之间存在明显权衡，且随着关键帧密度增加性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽支持多关键帧条件，但缺乏系统评估框架来衡量模型能否在保持视频质量的同时忠实再现指定的关键帧序列。

Method: 构建包含386个样本的基准，覆盖3个应用领域、2种视频结构、2种提示粒度、2种条件格式和4种关键帧密度；提出自动化评估框架，将关键帧执行分解为存在性、保真度、时序顺序、定位、持久性和唯一性6项指标，并通过MLLM结合专业感知模型评估整体视频质量。

Result: 当前模型在忠实执行关键帧与自然视频合成之间存在明显权衡；关键帧约束越密集性能越差；大多数开源模型无法将分镜网格输入解释为时序有序的关键帧序列。

Conclusion: KeyFrame-Compass揭示了现有视频生成模型在关键帧条件下的根本局限，为未来研究提供了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Video generation increasingly relies on keyframe-based workflows, where creators specify a sequence of reference images to guide generation. Although recent models support multi-keyframe conditioning, it remains unclear whether they can faithfully reproduce the prescribed keyframes while maintaining overall video quality. We present KeyFrame-Compass, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating keyframe-conditioned video generation. The benchmark contains 386 carefully curated samples spanning three application domains, two video structures, two prompt granularities, two conditioning formats, and four keyframe densities, enabling controlled analysis under diverse generation settings. We further introduce an automated evaluation framework that jointly measures keyframe execution and overall video quality. Specifically, we decompose keyframe execution into six complementary metrics covering presence, fidelity, temporal ordering, localization, persistence, and uniqueness, while assessing overall video quality through evidence-grounded MLLM judgments augmented with specialized perception models. Experiments on nine representative video generation systems reveal several fundamental limitations. Current models exhibit a clear trade-off between faithful keyframe execution and natural video synthesis. Their performance further degrades as keyframe constraints become denser and most open-source models also fail to interpret storyboard-grid inputs as temporally ordered keyframe sequences.

</details>


### [86] [SeeSE3: Emergence of 3D Space in Vision Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14228)
*Caroline Chen,Sayna Ebrahimi,Fedor Kitashov,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Leonidas Guibas,Viorica Pătrăucean,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究视觉基础模型是否在隐空间中构建了与3D欧几里得空间固有属性对齐的表征，并提出了一套从拓扑和几何角度评估这种对齐关系的探针方法。结果表明自监督视觉模型在正确探针下具有与三维欧氏空间高度相关的隐子空间，并据此提出了仅依赖隐空间进行视觉里程计和定位的"隐空间导航"技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要通过回归深度或法线等图像中心量来探测视觉特征的3D感知能力，但缺乏对视觉特征空间结构与三维欧氏变换群(SE(3))之间关系的系统性研究。作者希望从拓扑和几何两个层面，探究视觉基础模型是否自发地构建了反映三维欧氏空间内在属性的表征。

Method: 提出两套探针方法：1) 互近邻度量(Mutual Neighborhood Metric)，衡量特征空间邻域与空间拓扑之间的一致性；2) 庞加莱适配器(Poincaré Adapter)，测试静态场景中隐空间位移能否线性恢复相机运动的几何结构。通过在自监督视觉模型上应用这些探针进行评估。

Result: 自监督视觉模型在正确的探针方法下，其隐空间中存在与三维欧氏空间高度相关的子空间。基于此发现提出的"隐空间导航"技术，可以仅利用隐空间表示完成视觉里程计和定位，无需显式3D重建。

Conclusion: 自监督视觉模型虽未经过直接3D监督或主动代理训练，但已隐式地学习了与三维欧氏空间结构对齐的表征。利用这一特性，可以在隐空间中直接实现传统的3D视觉任务（如视觉里程计和定位），开辟了"隐空间导航"这一新方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we ask whether vision foundation models construct representations that reflect the intrinsic properties of 3D Euclidean space. Unlike previous works that probe 3D awareness of vision features by regressing image-centric quantities such as depth or normals, we investigate the relation between the structure of the space of visual features and the group of Euclidean transformations $SE(3)$. We propose a set of probes to evaluate this relation from both topological and geometric perspectives: a mutual neighborhood metric that measures the alignment between feature neighborhoods and spatial topology, and a Poincaré Adapter to test the linear accessibility of the geometry of camera motion from latent displacements in static scenes. We show that self-supervised vision models, which, in principle, have not been trained with direct 3D supervision or active agency, possess latent subspaces that are remarkably strongly correlated with three-dimensional Euclidean space, when probed correctly. Building on this insight we propose a new class of "Latent-Space Navigation" techniques that perform visual odometry and localization purely in the latent space, bypassing the need for explicit 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [87] [3D Lane Detection with Odometry for High-Speed Vehicle Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14248)
*Omoruyi Atekha,John Subosits,Marcus Greiff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对赛车场景的3D车道检测新数据集（>25万张多摄像头+惯性测量数据），并设计了支持近300Hz处理速度的多摄像头集成方法。通过结合惯性测量和里程计信息，相比BevLaneDet方法，F1分数提升3个百分点，近车MAE降低>30%，在实车部署中达到F1>0.9、横向MAE<0.18m的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车道检测研究主要针对常规驾驶场景，而赛车场景下车速更快、道路几何更极端，这一重要且挑战性的问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建了包含多摄像头图像和惯性测量数据的新数据集；提出支持近300Hz处理速度的改进方案；利用惯性测量进行预积分以回归道路几何；采用多摄像头集成方法进行预测。

Result: 相比BevLaneDet方法，加入里程计和集成预测后F1提升3点，近车MAE降低>30%；实车部署中F1>0.9，横向MAE<0.18m。

Conclusion: 惯性测量可作为有效的辅助模态用于预积分回归道路几何，多摄像头集成方法在保证高速处理（近300Hz）的同时显著提升了赛车场景下的车道检测性能。

Abstract: Lane boundary detection is a critical component in autonomous driving systems and has been rigorously studied in regular driving scenarios. However, it is less explored in vehicle racing, where the car moves at higher speeds across more extreme road geometries. To study this problem, we introduce a new dataset for 3D lane detection in racing, featuring >$250$k images from multiple camera feeds and inertial measurements taken with a Lexus LC 500 driving on a closed circuit. With this dataset, we compare various approaches to 3D lane detection and propose modifications that permit frames to be processed at rates of almost 300Hz while retaining high predictive performance in the racing application. This facilitates a multi-camera ensemble approach that is validated on hardware. We show that sensing modalities such as inertial measurements can be leveraged for pre-integration to regress road geometries over both cameras and time, yielding improvements in key metrics. Compared to methods such as BevLaneDet, adding odometry and ensemble predictions improves the F1 score by 3 points and reduces near-vehicle mean absolute errors (MAEs) by $>30 \%$. We show F1 scores $>$0.9 and lateral MAEs of $<$0.18m in vehicle deployments.

</details>


### [88] [MonteRET: AI Agent Enhancing Multimodal LLMs with Multi-granularity Knowledge Retrieval for Chest CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14264)
*Yi Lin,Yihao Ding,Elana Benishay,Elefterios Trikantzopoulos,David Nauheim,Hanley Ong,Jiang Bian,Hua Xu,Yuzhe Yang,George Shih,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonteRET是一种面向胸部CT报告生成的区域感知检索增强框架，通过融合全局与局部CT特征、基于预测病症和区域级视觉-语言对齐的知识检索以及知识引导的报告改写机制，在公开和外部数据集上显著提升了报告质量、语义相似性和临床效能，尤其减少发现遗漏，并获得了放射科住院医师的偏好评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床可信的胸部CT报告生成需要同时具备全容积理解和局部解剖发现的精确描述能力，现有方法在这两方面存在不足。

Method: MonteRET框架包含三个核心模块：1) 全局CT特征与区域级解剖表示的融合；2) 基于预测病症和区域级视觉-语言对齐的临床相关知识检索；3) 知识引导的报告改写智能体对初始报告进行精细化。

Result: 在RadGenome-ChestCT测试集（1,564例）和外部NYP-WCMC数据集（82例）上，MonteRET在报告质量、语义相似性和临床疗效指标上优于基线及多种SOTA方法，召回率提升最为显著（发现遗漏减少），放射科住院医师的人工评估也偏好MonteRET。

Conclusion: MonteRET通过区域感知和知识检索增强的方式有效提升了胸部CT报告生成的临床忠实度和完整性，尤其减少了发现遗漏问题。

Abstract: Automated chest CT report generation remains challenging because clinically faithful reporting requires both whole-volume understanding and accurate description of localized anatomical findings. Here we developed and retrospectively evaluated MonteRET, a region-aware retrieval-enhanced framework for generating chest CT findings sections. MonteRET integrates global CT features with region-level anatomical representations, retrieves clinically relevant knowledge using predicted medical conditions and region-level vision-language alignment, and refines initial reports through a knowledge-guided report rewriting agent. We trained our model on a public cohort with 24,128 CT scans from RadGenome-ChestCT. We evaluated MonteRET on the public RadGenome-ChestCT test set of 1,564 CT scans and an external cohort of 82 CT scans from NewYork-Presbyterian/Weill Cornell Medical Center. MonteRET improved report quality, semantic similarity, and clinical efficacy compared with a matched baseline and several state-of-the-art methods. Gains were most pronounced for recall, suggesting fewer omitted findings. Human expert evaluation by radiology residents also favored MonteRET.

</details>


### [89] [XCT-SAM: Sequential Parameter-Efficient Domain Adaptation of SAM for Industrial XCT Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14287)
*Md Mahedi Hasan,Md Mushfiqur Rahaman,Alan Pachkovskiy,Imtiaz Ahmed,Jeremy Dawson,Srinjoy Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XCT-SAM是一种针对增材制造XCT图像缺陷分割的序列化参数高效适配框架，通过先在合金微观结构数据集上微调Conv-LoRA适配器，再迁移到XCT图像，逐步缩小域间隙，仅训练约415万参数（冻结99%以上模型参数），在分布外测试中优于零样本SAM和其他域自适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造XCT图像缺陷分割面临严重的类别不平衡和扫描条件间的分布偏移，现有基础模型（如SAM）在自然图像上的预训练难以迁移到AM XCT领域，且缺乏标注的真实XCT数据。

Method: 提出XCT-SAM序列化参数高效适配框架：先在合金微观结构数据集上微调Conv-LoRA适配器（引入卷积空间归纳偏置到SAM骨干网络），再迁移到XCT图像；采用秩r=2的Conv-LoRA，仅训练约415万参数，冻结超过99%的模型参数。

Result: 在分布外CycleGAN-XCT基准和真实NIST XCT扫描上，XCT-SAM在IoU和Dice分数上一致优于零样本SAM及其他域自适应SAM基线。

Conclusion: 中间域自适应结合参数高效适配器对工业XCT缺陷分割有效，能逐步缩小域间隙，提升分割性能。

Abstract: Defect segmentation in additive manufacturing (AM) X-ray computed tomography (XCT) images remains challenging due to severe class imbalance and large distribution shifts across scan conditions. Although recent foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) provide strong general-purpose segmentation priors, their natural-image pre-training transfers poorly to the AM XCT domain, where defects appear as subtle non-semantic microstructural anomalies. Moreover, adapting SAM to the AM domain is further limited by the large domain gap and scarcity of labeled real XCT data. We present XCT-SAM, a sequential parameter-efficient adaptation framework for AM XCT defect segmentation. Instead of adapting SAM directly from natural images to XCT data, we first fine-tune Conv-LoRA adapters on an alloy-microstructure dataset and subsequently transfer the adapted model to XCT images, progressively bridging the domain gap. Using Conv-LoRA adapters with rank r=2, the framework injects convolutional spatial inductive bias into SAM's backbone while training approximately 4.15M parameters and keeping over 99% of the model frozen. We evaluate XCT-SAM on out-of-distribution CycleGAN-XCT benchmarks and real-world NIST XCT scans. Across both settings, XCT-SAM consistently outperforms zero-shot SAM and other domain-adapted SAM baselines, achieving the best overall IoU and Dice scores. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of intermediate domain adaptation with parameter-efficient adapters for industrial XCT defect segmentation. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Mahedi-61/XCT-SAM.git

</details>


### [90] [Traceback Translators Against Forgetting in Continual Fake Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12569)
*Enrico Gottardis,Mattia Tamiazzo,Simone Milani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于域翻译器（traceback translator network）的遗忘鲁棒解决方案，通过在冻结的检测器内将新特征空间映射回原始特征空间，在最小化计算开销的同时保持对旧数据的检测性能，有效应对伪造语音检测中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型不断进步，伪造语音检测器面临挑战；传统持续学习在更新模型时会导致灾难性遗忘，即新数据上的性能提升以牺牲旧数据上的检测精度为代价。

Method: 提出一种基于traceback translator network的域翻译方法，在保持原有检测器参数冻结的情况下，通过翻译网络将新域的特征空间映射回原始域的特征空间，从而实现持续学习而不遗忘。

Result: 该方法相比传统重训练策略实现了更高的检测率，同时显著降低了计算开销，并保持了在先前数据上的检测精度。

Conclusion: traceback translator网络是一种有效的遗忘鲁棒持续学习方案，能在不牺牲旧数据性能的前提下实现伪造语音检测模型的高效更新。

Abstract: Fake speech detectors are increasingly challenged by the development of new and more accurate generative models. To cope with this problem, continual learning techniques are nowadays widely considered feasible strategies for updating models to new datasets, but they also lead to decreased performance on previously seen samples (catastrophic forgetting). In this work, we propose a forgetting-resilient solution based on the adoption of domain translators within a frozen detector, which remaps the new feature spaces into the original ones by means of a traceback translator network. Experimental results show that this strategy enables the achievement of high detection rates with respect to traditional retraining, while minimizing the computational effort and preserving the detection accuracy on previous data.

</details>


### [91] [DCVC-MB: Neural B-Frame Video Compression using State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14305)
*Arjun Arora,Calvin-Khang Ta,Carlos Restrepo-Galeano,Kruthi Murali,Naga Akhil E S,Arunkumar Mohananchettiar,Jay Shingala,Tong Shao,Peng Yin,Sean McCarthy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCVC-Mamba (DCVC-MB) 是一个基于状态空间模型的双向时序预测神经视频编码框架，专为B帧编码设计，通过IBP帧策略、时空融合模型和熵感知跳过机制，在BD-rate上相比现有神经视频编码器平均降低8.98%，相比VTM-19.0-LDP和VTM-19.0-RA分别提升30.45%和1.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经视频编码在B帧编码场景中性能不足，需要更高效的时序预测与编码跳过的机制来提升压缩效率。

Method: 提出DCVC-MB框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) IBP帧策略用于低延迟B帧编码；(2) 基于状态空间模型(SSM)的时空融合模型实现双向时序预测；(3) 熵感知跳过机制选择性省略部分潜变量的编码以降低熵编码时间。此外还引入两种推理时策略来进一步提升压缩性能。

Result: DCVC-MB在BD-rate上相比现有神经视频编码器平均降低8.98%，相比VTM-19.0-LDP和VTM-19.0-RA(Inter-GoP=16)基准分别提升30.45%和1.81%。

Conclusion: DCVC-MB在B帧编码任务上显著优于现有神经视频编码器和传统编码标准，推动了神经视频压缩领域的发展。

Abstract: In this paper we propose DCVC-Mamba (DCVC-MB), a neural video codec framework for B-frame coding. Our approach incorporates an IBP frame strategy for low-delay B-frame coding, a spatio-temporal fusion model based on state-space models for bidirectional temporal prediction, and an entropy-aware skipping mechanism that selectively omits coding certain latents to reduce entropy coding times. In addition to our model contributions we also implement two inference-time strategies that enhance compression performance. Experimental evaluation shows that DCVC-MB compares favorably to existing NVCs and traditional codecs. The method demonstrates BD-rate reductions of up to $8.98\%$ on average compared to prior neural video codecs, and improvements of up to $30.45\%$ and $1.81\%$ over the VTM-19.0-LDP and VTM-19.0-RA(Inter-GoP=16) benchmarks, respectively, contributing to advances in neural video compression.

</details>


### [92] [SD-MAR: Multi-image Analytical Reasoning via Synthetic Data and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14333)
*Shiyu Yuan,Sourav Sanjukta Bhabesh,Zhe Wang,Dmitriy Bespalov,Wesley Rose,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SD-MAR框架，通过受控扰动构建配对视觉场景，生成多图像分析推理任务，并采用GRPO-lite结合反向折扣分配（BDA）的强化学习方法训练VLM，在Qwen2.5-VL-7B和InternVL3-8B上域内准确率提升达36.95%，同时保持或提升域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在需要多视觉状态分析推理的任务（如多图比较、变化检测、多步视觉推理）中能力有限，而现有基准很少同时要求显式视觉比较和分析推理，导致该能力未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建SD-MAR框架，通过受控扰动生成配对视觉场景及推理任务（语义变化归因、定量比较）；2) 提出GRPO-lite with BDA，去除KL正则化以强化策略优化，并将更大奖励权重分配给后期推理步骤。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-7B和InternVL3-8B在SD-MAR上域内准确率提升高达36.95%；Qwen2.5-VL-7B在SD-MAR基准上超越GPT-4.1；域外泛化保持稳定（MME、MMMU-Pro、MathVista误差≤1%），MMBench提升达4%；LLM-as-judge评估显示逻辑连贯性和解释质量一致性提升。

Conclusion: SD-MAR框架结合GRPO-lite with BDA强化学习方法能有效提升VLM的多图像分析推理能力，且不牺牲域外泛化性能，为多模态分析推理任务提供了有效的训练与评估方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong perceptual abilities but remain limited in tasks requiring analytical reasoning across multiple visual states, such as multi-image comparison, change detection, and multi-step visual inference. These capabilities are critical for real-world multimodal applications where reasoning must be grounded in systematic differences between visual contexts. However, existing benchmarks rarely require both explicit visual comparison and analytical reasoning, leaving this capability underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce SD-MAR (Synthetic Data for Multi-image Analytical Reasoning), a framework for training and evaluating VLMs on multi-image analytical reasoning. SD-MAR constructs paired visual scenarios through controlled perturbations and generates reasoning tasks spanning semantic change attribution and quantitative comparison. We further train VLMs using GRPO-lite with Backward Discounted Allocation (BDA), a reinforcement learning approach that removes KL regularization to encourage stronger policy optimization while allocating greater credit to the later reasoning steps where analytical conclusions are formed. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3-8B show that GRPO-lite fine-tuning on SD-MAR improves in-domain accuracy by up to 36.95%, with Qwen2.5-VL-7B outperforming GPT-4.1 on the SD-MAR benchmark. Importantly, out-of-domain generalization is preserved or improved: performance remains within 1% on MME, MMMU-Pro, and MathVista, while improving by up to 4% on MMBench. LLM-as-judge evaluation further demonstrates consistent improvements in logical coherence and explanation quality across both models.

</details>


### [93] [MixCompress: Mixture of Experts for Variable Rate Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14334)
*Calvin-Khang Ta,Praneet Singh,Tong Shao,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixCompress提出基于稀疏结构特化（MoE+MoD）的统一变码率图像压缩框架，通过动态容量缩放解决梯度冲突，在计算高效性上达到新的Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变码率方法因共享骨干网络在低码率平滑梯度与高码率高频细节保持之间存在根本性梯度冲突，导致性能次优。

Method: 提出MixCompress框架，包含：(1) 稀疏门控MoE路由缓解梯度冲突；(2) Mixture-of-Depths动态扩展模型容量以适应高码率需求；(3) 条件辅助变换（CAT）实现子带能量动态调制。

Result: MixCompress不仅匹配了单独优化的单码率基线，甚至能超越它们，在计算高效图像编码上建立了新的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏结构特化与动态容量缩放，MixCompress有效解决了变码率压缩中的特征纠缠问题，实现了计算效率与率失真性能的双重提升。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) is bottlenecked by the need to store independent models for each rate-distortion operating point. Existing variable bit-rate (VBR) methods aim to reduce this overhead via dense parameter modulation, but forcing a shared backbone to approximate divergent mappings causes severe feature entanglement. Specifically, low-rate smoothing gradients inherently conflict with the preservation of high-frequency textural details, leading to sub-optimal performance. To resolve this, we propose MixCompress, a unified VBR framework based on sparse structural specialization. While sparsely gated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing successfully mitigates gradient conflict, it operates on a fixed computational budget. To address the increased representational demands of higher bit-rates we introduce a Mixture-of-Depths (MoD) extension to dynamically scale model capacity. Combined with Conditional Auxiliary Transforms (CAT) for dynamic sub-band energy modulation, our hierarchical framework effectively dynamically scales capacity. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that MixCompress not only matches individually optimized single-rate baselines but can even surpass them, establishing a new Pareto frontier for computationally efficient image coding.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond scalar losses: calibrating segmentation models via gradient vector field surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14338)
*Laurin Lux,Alexander H. Berger,Moritz Knolle,Daniel Rückert,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对区域损失函数（如Dice损失）导致模型过度自信和校准不良问题的梯度"手术"方法，通过在梯度向量场上添加一个与预测误差线性相关的缩放因子来缓解校准问题，在2D和3D医学分割任务中验证了其有效性且不影响预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域损失函数（如Dice损失）在类别和区域不平衡的分割任务中表现优异，但会导致模型校准不良，产生过度自信的预测。这在医学影像应用（如肿瘤切除边界界定）中阻碍了临床采纳，需要解决校准问题。

Method: 从梯度视角分析了区域损失函数导致过度自信的原因，提出在损失函数的偏导数上添加一个因子，使梯度幅度与预测误差呈线性缩放关系，即对梯度向量场进行"手术"干预。

Result: 在2D和3D医学分割任务上的实证评估表明，该方法能有效改善模型校准问题，同时在与任何区域损失函数联合使用时保持高预测精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的梯度"手术"是一种简单而有效的干预手段，能够在不牺牲分割精度的前提下缓解区域损失函数导致的校准不良问题，有望促进医学影像分割模型的临床采纳。

Abstract: Region-based loss functions, such as the Dice loss, have established themselves as the de facto standard for highly class- and region-imbalanced segmentation tasks. However, models trained using region-based loss functions are notoriously miscalibrated and typically yield over-confident predictions. In medical imaging applications, such as defining tumor resection margins, this miscalibration is hindering clinical adoption. In this work, we outline a novel gradient perspective on this overconfidence and show how it affects region-based loss functions. We propose a "surgery" on the gradient vector field as a simple, yet effective intervention to mitigate calibration issues. This surgery adds a factor to the loss's partial derivative, scaling the gradient's magnitude linearly with the prediction error. In empirical evaluations across 2D and 3D medical segmentation tasks, we demonstrate the effectiveness of this intervention while maintaining high prediction accuracy when used in conjunction with any region-based loss function.

</details>


### [95] [Dynamic Manipulation Hypergraphs for HAR: Beyond Pairwise Relations: Dynamic Manipulation Hypergraphs for Vision-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14350)
*Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态操作超图框架，通过高阶关系建模多实体配置，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR和Assembly101上显著优于成对图和静态超图，HO-F1分别提升6.9/9.5和4.4/5.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图方法使用成对边，会将协调事件拆解为孤立的二元关系，无法有效建模手、物体、工具和支撑面等多实体间的协同演变关系。

Method: 动态操作超图框架：1) 多实体编码（外观、空间、运动、语义角色特征）；2) 超边候选实例化与排序（基于邻近、接触、运动耦合谓词）；3) 超图推理网络（节点-超边双向消息传递+时间注意力）；4) 输出类无关的超边重要性分数。

Result: 在EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR上HO-F1提升6.9% vs 成对图、4.4% vs 静态超图；Assembly101上提升9.5%和5.8%；ARCTIC定性分析显示高排名超边与接触密集型操作区间对应。

Conclusion: 时变高阶关系建模对细粒度操作活动识别具有重要价值，超边重要性分数可识别模型关注的关键实体配置和时间区间。

Abstract: Fine-grained manipulation recognition requires modeling evolving relations among hands, objects, tools, and supporting surfaces. Conventional graph-based methods use pairwise edges that can fragment a coordinated event into disconnected binary relations. We propose a dynamic manipulation hypergraph framework that represents multi-entity configurations as higher-order relational units. At each temporal step, relevant entities are encoded using appearance, spatial, motion, and semantic-role features. Hyperedge candidates are instantiated and ranked using proximity, contact, and motion-coupling predicates. A hypergraph reasoning network performs node-to-hyperedge and hyperedge-to-node message passing, followed by temporal attention over the evolving interaction structure. The framework provides class-agnostic hyperedge-importance scores that identify entity configurations and temporal intervals emphasized by the model without treating them as causal explanations. Quantitative evaluation is conducted on EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR and Assembly101 under an annotation-assisted entity-localization protocol. Video-only and entity-based methods provide contextual comparisons, while a matched pairwise graph and a static hypergraph serve as the principal controlled baselines because they use identical entity inputs and comparable relational settings. The proposed method improves HO-F1 over the matched pairwise graph by 6.9 percentage points on EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR and 9.5 points on Assembly101, and exceeds the static hypergraph by 4.4 and 5.8 points, respectively. Qualitative analysis on ARCTIC further shows correspondence between highly ranked hyperedges and contact-rich manipulation intervals. These results demonstrate the value of time-varying higher-order relational modeling for fine-grained manipulation activity recognition.

</details>


### [96] [On Success and Simplicity: A Second Look at Transferable Vision-Language Attack Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14974)
*Yuchen Ren,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Bo Yang,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SimVLA，一种更简单但更高效的可迁移对抗攻击方法，针对视觉-语言预训练模型，通过解决跨模态交互不当和操作冗余问题，在Flickr30k数据集上实现了比SOTA基线高8.01%-14.71%的R@1迁移性，同时仅消耗约35.73%的时间和46.26%的显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对VLPMs的可迁移攻击方法通常采用复杂的损失函数或多阶段文本/图像攻击流程，但作者发现三个被忽视的问题：不恰当的跨模态交互和过多冗余操作。因此希望通过简化攻击流程来提升效果和效率。

Method: 提出SimVLA（Simple Vision-Language Attack）流水线，识别并解决了三个被忽视的问题，包括引入跨模态词识别（cross-modal word identification）作为领域知识，摒弃复杂的损失函数和多阶段设计。

Result: 在四个数据集和三个下游任务上验证了有效性。在Flickr30k图文检索任务中，SimVLA在R@1迁移性上超过SOTA基线8.01%-14.71%，同时仅消耗约35.73%的时间和46.26%的最大显存。

Conclusion: 利用领域知识（如跨模态词识别）比盲目追求复杂操作更重要，SimVLA可作为简单有效的基线框架供未来研究扩展。

Abstract: Vision-Language Pre-training Models (VLPMs) are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Recent transferable attacks on VLPMs have followed a common pipeline with complicated loss functions or multi-stage text/image attacks. However, in this paper, we demonstrate that such a sophisticated attack pipeline can be simpler yet more successful. Specifically, we identify three previously overlooked issues caused by inappropriate cross-modal interactions and excessive operations. To address them, we propose the Simple Vision-Language Attack (SimVLA) pipeline, which observably improves transferability and efficiency. Experiments on four datasets and three downstream tasks validate the superiority of our pipeline. For instance, on Flickr30k text-image retrieval dataset, our SimVLA outperforms the SOTA baseline in R@1 transferability by 8.01\%-14.71\%, while consuming only about 35.73\% of the time and 46.26\% of the max VRAM. Overall, the superiority of our SimVLA highlights the importance of leveraging domain knowledge (e.g., our proposed cross-modal word identification), while blindly pursuing intricate operations (e.g, complex loss functions and redundant multi-stage designs) may even be harmful. We hope our SimVLA can serve as a simple yet effective backbone for future extensions. Code is available at https://github.com/RYC-98/SimVLA.

</details>


### [97] [$K$-NeAS: Scalable Multi-Material CT Reconstruction Using Neural SDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14415)
*Daksh K. Shah,Emmanouil Nikolakakis,Razvan V. Marinescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: K-NeAS提出了一种统一的可扩展架构，用于自动、多材料的稀疏视图CT表面重建，通过共享潜在骨干网络、可微分的K材料顺序软选择器、GMM自动衰减边界估计和辅助浮动体损失，在四个临床CBCT数据集上实现了优于单材料基线的3D体素保真度和稀疏采样鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式场景表示方法在稀疏视图CT重建中存在局限性：手动调整衰减边界、仅支持刚性双材料约束，无法扩展到任意数量的重叠组织。

Method: 1) 用共享潜在骨干网络替代独立材料网络；2) 提出全可微分的K材料顺序软选择器建模任意数量重叠组织；3) 使用高斯混合模型自动估计衰减边界；4) 引入调度辅助浮动体损失减少几何幻觉。

Result: 在四个临床CBCT数据集上，K=3材料时腹部区域3D PSNR达33.28 dB（相比单材料基线提升1.88 dB）；在5视图和10视图的稀疏采样条件下，3D PSNR最高提升1.17 dB。

Conclusion: K-NeAS成功实现了任意材料数量的可扩展多材料表面重建，消除了手动调参需求，并在多组织和稀疏采样场景下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Computed Tomography (CT) carries significant ionizing radiation risks, driving the need for sparse-view reconstruction. Implicit scene representations (ISRs) address this by recovering continuous volumetric attenuation fields directly from sparse projections, and recent geometry-aware extensions jointly model surface geometry alongside attenuation to improve fidelity and enable clean tissue segmentation without manual thresholding. However, these methods remain limited by manually tuned attenuation bounds and rigid two-material constraints. This paper proposes $K$-NeAS, a unified and scalable architecture for automated, multi-material surface reconstruction. We replace independent material networks with a shared latent backbone and introduce a fully differentiable $K$-material sequential soft selector to model an arbitrary number of overlapping tissues. To eliminate manual tuning, we automate attenuation bounding using a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) and implement a scheduled auxiliary floater loss to mitigate geometric hallucinations common under extreme sparsity. Evaluated across four clinical Cone-Beam CT (CBCT) datasets, $K$-NeAS successfully scales to arbitrary material counts, achieving superior 3D volumetric fidelity at $K=3$ materials on complex multi-tissue regions such as the Abdomen ($33.28\text{ dB}$ 3D PSNR vs. $31.40\text{ dB}$ single-material NeAS baseline, a $+1.88\text{ dB}$ improvement). Furthermore, our model exhibits enhanced robustness under sparse-sampling conditions, outperforming baseline 3D PSNR by up to $1.17\text{ dB}$ under 5- and 10-view constraints.

</details>


### [98] [Emergent Region-Level Facial Correspondence in Frozen Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14423)
*Izaldein Al-Zyoud,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现冻结的DINOv3自监督视觉模型无需人脸特定训练，即可在人类面部建立区域级对应关系，在跨身份匹配和时序跟踪上表现优异，且中间层特征比最终层更具判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自监督模型能对齐通用物体的部件对应关系，但人类面部具有共享全局布局而身份外观差异大的特殊性，现有研究未明确冻结的自监督特征是否能在不经过人脸训练的情况下建立区域级面部对应关系。

Method: 使用冻结的DINOv3 ViT-L/16 patch嵌入作为特征，仅以FaRL作为面部部件标注接口（无训练），在CelebDF-v2的200个真实视频上评估跨身份最近邻匹配和时序标签传播性能，并与CLIP ViT-L/14及随机基线对比。

Result: DINOv3在跨身份匹配上达到83.0%区域级语义准确率（随机基线23.0%），时序跟踪准确率95.5%（无需时序模块）；中间层（block 18）的同区域vs跨区域判别比达4.93x，远高于最终层的1.48x；相比CLIP，整体优势不大但在解剖学区域上高出16.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 冻结的DINOv3可作为区域级面部对应的强大零样本表示，中间层自监督特征最适合密集面部分析，图像级对比学习（如CLIP）只能捕捉粗略面部布局而无法获取精细解剖学身份对应。

Abstract: Frozen self-supervised vision models can align parts of generic objects, but it remains unclear whether this correspondence extends to human faces, where global layout is shared while identity-specific appearance varies sharply. We test whether frozen DINOv3 features define a region-level facial coordinate system: a feature space in which eyes, brows, nose, mouth, skin, and hair remain distinguishable across people and across time without face-specific training. Using DINOv3 ViT-L/16 patch embeddings and FaRL only as a face-part labeling interface, we evaluate cross-identity nearest-neighbor matching and temporal label propagation on 200 CelebDF-v2 real videos. DINOv3 achieves 83.0% region-level semantic accuracy under unconstrained cross-identity matching, compared with a 23.0% area-weighted random baseline, and 95.5% temporal tracking accuracy without a learned temporal module. A no-FaRL control collapses to 0.9%, showing that FaRL supplies semantic initialization while DINOv3 supplies dense spatial correspondence. The strongest correspondence appears at an intermediate layer: block 18 gives a 4.93x same-region versus cross-region discrimination ratio, compared with 1.48x at the final block. Against CLIP ViT-L/14, DINOv3 shows only a small aggregate advantage but a +16.8 pp gain on anatomical regions, indicating that image-level contrastive supervision captures coarse facial layout but not fine-grained anatomical identity. These results establish frozen DINOv3 as a strong zero-shot representation for region-level facial correspondence and identify intermediate self-supervised features as the most useful layer for dense face analysis.

</details>


### [99] [Cotton-SF YOLO: Learning Structural and Frequency Cues for Early Cotton Square Detection in Complex Field Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14445)
*Chengjia Zhang,Yu Li,Feiri Ali,Yan Zhang,Xin Chen,Longke He,Daokun Ma,Liting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对棉田复杂环境下棉铃早期检测存在的小目标、遮挡、模糊、光照变化和低对比度等问题，本文提出了一种基于YOLO26m的任务导向框架Cotton-SF YOLO，通过引入动态蛇形卷积（DySnakeConv）增强不规则边缘特征提取，并在C2f结构中设计频域特征调制模块（FFM）来增强判别性边缘和纹理线索、减少叶片背景干扰。在自建数据集上，该模型mAP50、mAP50:95和召回率分别达到0.8196、0.4942和0.7939，较基线YOLO26m提升了1.25%、3.45%和2.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂田间环境下早期棉铃检测面临小目标、遮挡、模糊、光照变化大、与叶片对比度低等挑战，现有研究对此探索不足，亟需针对这些难题设计专用检测方法。

Method: 基于YOLO26m构建Cotton-SF YOLO框架，包括：(1) 动态蛇形卷积（Dynamic Snake Convolution）用于适应性地提取可变形边缘特征；(2) 频域特征调制模块（FFM），将频谱增强集成到C2f结构中，重新校准频域表示，增强判别性边缘和纹理线索，同时降低复杂棉花叶片背景的干扰。

Result: 在自建人工标注的田间数据集上，模型mAP50、mAP50:95和召回率分别为0.8196、0.4942和0.7939，相比基线YOLO26m分别提升1.25%、3.45%和2.96%。消融实验和可视化结果表明结构线索与频域线索具有互补效果，联合使用时性能最佳。

Conclusion: 所提出的Cotton-SF YOLO通过结合结构感知的动态蛇形卷积与频域特征调制，有效解决了复杂田间环境下小目标棉铃的检测难题，为棉花生长监测和精准栽培管理提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Cotton squares are important phenotypic indicators of the early reproductive growth of cotton, and automatic field detection of cotton squares provides an important basis for cotton growth monitoring and precision cultivation management. However, early cotton square detection in complex field environments remains insufficiently explored, as cotton squares are small, frequently occluded, easily blurred, subject to illumination variations, and exhibit low contrast against surrounding cotton leaves. To address these challenges, we propose a task-oriented framework based on YOLO26m, named Cotton-SF YOLO, for cotton square detection under natural field conditions. To improve the perception of small and irregular cotton square boundaries, we introduce Dynamic Snake Convolution into the detector, enabling adaptive extraction of deformable edge features. Furthermore, a frequency-domain feature modulation module is designed by incorporating spectral enhancement into the C2f structure, which recalibrate frequency-domain representations and strengthen discriminative edge and texture cues while reducing interference from complex cotton leaf backgrounds. Trained and evaluated on our newly constructed and annotated field dataset with manually annotated cotton squares, the proposed model achieves mAP$_{50}$, mAP$_{50:95}$, and recall values of 0.8196, 0.4942, and 0.7939, improving over the baseline YOLO26m by 1.25%, 3.45%, and 2.96%, respectively. Ablation experiments and visualization demonstrate that the best performance is achieved with the complementary effects of structural and frequency cues.

</details>


### [100] [G$^2$SR: Geometric Methods for Fast and Memory-Efficient Gaussian-based Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14470)
*Dasong Gao,Vivienne Sze,Sertac Karaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G2SR提出了一种轻量级的高斯表面重建方法，利用2D高斯斑点的跨视图对应关系，通过分析几何三角化快速重建3D场景，在保持高几何精度的同时将GPU内存降低5-107倍，重建速度达69-89 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量RGB图像进行表面重建是移动机器人在线3D感知的关键需求，但现有方法存在两难：3DGS虽表示能力强却因少视图不适定性而产生漂浮物伪影；端到端方法虽可解决歧义但依赖大型Transformer网络，计算和内存开销大且泛化性差。

Method: G2SR采用混合架构：轻量级神经前端在图像平面上检测和跟踪2D高斯斑点，利用多视图几何约束提供对应关系；解析几何后端将这些2D对应关系通过三角化生成度量尺度的3D高斯斑点，从而将不适定问题转化为适定的几何重建问题。

Result: 在ScanNet、Replica和DTU数据集上，G2SR的几何精度达到或超过最先进的端到端方法，在384×512分辨率下，2-3视图输入时重建速度达69-89 FPS，GPU内存仅需203 MB（比对比方法降低5-107倍）。

Conclusion: G2SR通过将高斯表面重建分解为轻量级2D跟踪和解析3D三角化，在保持高几何精度的同时实现了极低的计算和内存开销，为移动机器人上的在线高斯表面重建提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Few-view surface reconstruction recovers the visible surfaces of a scene from a few posed RGB images, providing the 3D models that robots need to explore and interact online. On mobile platforms, the reconstruction must be fast and geometrically accurate while keeping a small memory footprint to ensure safe and efficient operation. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers a high-fidelity scene representation, but building it from a few views is ill-posed, as many distinct surfaces reproduce the same images, making traditional photometric methods prone to "floater" artifacts. End-to-end methods resolve the ambiguity by regressing splats with large, usually Transformer-based, networks that require heavy compute and memory while generalizing poorly to new scenes. We propose G2SR, which exploits a well-posed core of the task: given cross-view 2D splat correspondences, 3D splats follow analytically from multi-view geometry. G2SR employs a lightweight neural frontend to detect and track 2D Gaussian splats on the image plane and an analytic backend to triangulate each into a metric-scale 3D splat. On ScanNet, Replica, and DTU, G2SR matches or exceeds the geometric accuracy of state-of-the-art end-to-end methods while running at 69-89 reconstructions per second within 203 MB of GPU memory (5-107x less) for 2- and 3-view inputs at 384 x 512 resolution, offering a practical path to online Gaussian-based surface reconstruction.

</details>


### [101] [Immediate 3D Gaussian Splat Reconstruction of Unordered Input with Global Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14481)
*Andreas Meuleman,Linus Franke,Boris Zhestiankin,Camille Montemagni,George Drettakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的即时反馈重建方案，通过重利用视觉地点识别模型和共视图实现无序序列快速匹配，引入基于共视图的聚类方法实现无需顺序输入的闭环检测，并采用渐进式层次结构实现大规模场景扩展，在多个数据集上实现了即时反馈的高质量3DGS重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS重建方法中，连续图像序列通常需要结合无序拍摄以覆盖场景，但SfM虽能处理此类数据却需全部图像且计算成本高，增量式方法（如SLAM）能提供即时反馈但无法处理无序输入。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供首个既能处理无序序列又能提供即时反馈的3DGS重建方案。

Method: 1) 利用视觉地点识别模型和共视图实现无序序列的快速图像匹配；2) 高效查找高度连接的关键帧以提升质量；3) GPU优化与高斯原语精心放置实现快速局部重建；4) 基于共视图的聚类方法实现高效闭环检测，无需顺序输入；5) 渐进式层次结构实现大规模场景扩展。

Result: 在多个数据集（包含数千张输入图像）上实现了即时反馈的3DGS重建，且视觉质量良好。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了针对辐射场重建的无序序列即时反馈方案，通过共视图驱动的匹配、闭环和层次化扩展，在保持全局一致性的同时提供了良好的视觉质量与可扩展性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the method of choice for reconstructing and real-time rendering of captured scenes. To capture a scene with good visual quality, continuous image sequences are usually combined with out-of-order shots for better scene coverage. Structure from motion can reconstruct such captures, but only after they are all available and often with high computational cost. Incremental reconstruction methods -- often derived from SLAM solutions -- provide immediate feedback, but cannot handle the out-of-order capture we require. We provide the first immediate feedback solution for such radiance field capture that provides global consistency. We first introduce a method for fast matching in out-of-order sequences, by repurposing visual place recognition models and a covisibility graph, and provide an efficient way to find highly connected keyframes, improving quality even for ordered sequences. We show how these steps -- together with GPU optimization and careful Gaussian primitive placement -- provide fast local reconstruction, in our challenging radiance field reconstruction case. We then introduce a novel cluster-based method, again using the covisibility graph, to provide efficient loop closure that does not require sequential input. Finally, to handle large scenes in our context, we introduce a progressive hierarchy that allows our method to scale to large environments, without compromising efficiency. Our results show we provide immediate feedback 3DGS reconstruction with good visual quality in several datasets, with up to thousands of input images.

</details>


### [102] [Reinforcing Egocentric Spatial Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models via Ego Scene Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14497)
*Chi Kit Wong,Ye Pan,Yuanhuiyi Lyu,Xu Zheng,Zidong Cao,Lutao Jiang,Zixin Zhang,Huiyu Zhou,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ego Scene Augmentation (ESA)框架，通过Ego-element Graph增强多模态大模型在自我中心视觉问答中的空间感知能力，在EgoTextVQA基准上 indoor 提升8.14%，outdoor 提升8.72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在复杂自我中心场景中空间推理能力不足，缺乏有效的自我中心空间感知机制。

Method: 构建Ego-element Graph封装并整合视觉基础模型提取的自我中心空间特征，以此增强MLLMs的空间感知能力。

Result: 在EgoTextVQA基准上，indoor设置提升8.14%，outdoor设置提升8.72%，其中购物子集表现最优。

Conclusion: ESA框架通过图结构中介表示有效增强了自我中心视觉问答中的空间感知能力，显著提升了MLLMs在复杂场景下的表现。

Abstract: Egocentric Visual Question Answering (VQA) has attracted widespread attention as an important task for enabling Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to interact with the real world. However, existing MLLMs struggle to perform effective spatial reasoning in complex egocentric scenes due to their limited spatial perception capabilities. To this end, we introduce Ego Scene Augmentation (ESA), an egocentric spatial perception framework, which actively enhances the spatial perception capabilities from the egocentric perspective, powered by the proposed Ego-element Graph. Our core insight is leveraging the Ego-element Graph as an intermediary representation to augment the egocentric spatial perception of MLLMs via visual foundational models. Specifically, we 1) construct the Ego-element Graph, which encapsulates and integrates egocentric spatial features enabled by visual foundational models; 2) enhance the spatial perception capabilities of MLLMs via the Ego-element Graph for ego-perspective scenes. Our proposed ESA framework presents significant performance improvement on the EgoTextVQA benchmark. We achieve an 8.14% gain on the indoor setting and an 8.72% gain on the outdoor setting. Furthermore, our ESA shows the most impressive performance improvement in the shopping subset of the indoor setting. The project code is publicly available.

</details>


### [103] [Multi-Scale ViT Inference with Habitat-Fit Priors and kNN Retrieval for Multi-Species Plant Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14509)
*Alper Erten,Murilo Gustineli,Adrian Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DS@GT ARC在PlantCLEF 2026竞赛中的第三名解决方案，针对植被样方图像中的多物种植物识别任务。核心流水线基于微调DINOv2 ViT-L/14分类器，结合多尺度图块分解、FAISS kNN检索融合、时间融合、生境适应降权及地理掩码，最终在私有榜上达到0.43902 macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决植被样方图像（~3000x3000像素）中多物种植物识别的挑战，即仅使用单标签图像训练，但需预测高分辨率图像中所有存在的物种。

Method: 1) 多尺度图块分解：将样方图像分解为多尺度图块，使用微调DINOv2 ViT-L/14分类器逐图块预测；2) FAISS kNN检索：融合检索结果与分类器预测；3) 时间融合：对多次访问的样方进行源感知时序融合；4) 生境适应降权：注入地理和海拔先验；5) 南欧地理掩码；6) 尝试了跨区域Transformer噪声师生蒸馏和标签查询Transformer解码器，但未取得效果。

Result: 私有榜macro-F1为0.43902（第三名），公开榜0.51096；未选择的配置在私有集上超过0.45；生境适应降权和多尺度聚合是消融实验中最有效的组件。

Conclusion: 基于DINOv2的多尺度图块分类加知识注入的流水线在植物物种识别任务中有效；而基于Transformer的复杂训练方法未带来提升，表明简单且精心设计的推理策略在当前任务中更具优势。

Abstract: This paper describes DS@GT ARC's third-place solution to the PlantCLEF 2026 challenge on multi-species plant identification in vegetation quadrat images, where systems must predict every species present in high-resolution (~3000 x 3000 pixel) plot photographs while training only on single-label images of individual plants. The pipeline is built around a fine-tuned DINOv2 ViT-L/14 classifier applied over a multi-scale tile decomposition of each quadrat, with per-tile predictions blended with a FAISS kNN retriever and post-processed by source-aware temporal fusion across repeated plot visits, a habitat-fit demotion that injects geographic and altitude priors from the training data, and a South-Western Europe geographic mask. Habitat-fit demotion and multi-scale aggregation are the largest individual contributors in the ablations. Two complementary training-centric directions, a cross-region transformer with noisy-student distillation on the LUCAS dataset and a label-as-query transformer decoder over synthetic CLS-domain pseudo-quadrats, yielded null results. An inference-time augmentation with instance-aware segmentation crops also did not improve performance. The selected submission reaches a private-leaderboard macro-F1 of 0.43902 (third place; public 0.51096); an unselected configuration of the same pipeline scored above 0.45 on the private set. Code: https://github.com/dsgt-arc/plantclef-2026.

</details>


### [104] [Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting Data Using GPU-friendly Graphics Texture Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14513)
*Amir Said,Randall Rauwendaal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用GPU硬件加速的纹理压缩方案（BC1/BC7）压缩3D高斯溅射中的球谐系数，通过局部分组重排和比特率控制策略，在保持随机访问和并行解码能力的同时显著减少内存需求，且视觉质量损失可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射（3DGS）使用球谐函数（SH）建模视角相关颜色，导致每个图元需要大量SH系数，内存占用巨大。现有压缩方法未充分利用现代GPU的并行解码能力和专用硬件加速。

Method: 利用GPU硬件支持的纹理压缩格式（BC1/BC7）压缩SH颜色系数；通过局部图元分组和按颜色重排序来提升压缩效率；引入保持随机访问的比特率控制策略，支持大规模并行化渲染。

Result: 基于BC1和BC7格式的实验表明，该方法在GPU上实现高效解压缩，渲染3DGS场景的视觉质量退化可忽略或几乎不可察觉。

Conclusion: 所提纹理压缩方案比2D纹理更有效地压缩颜色系数，且通过硬件加速的并行解码在不牺牲渲染性能的前提下大幅降低内存需求。

Abstract: Techniques for modeling 3D scenes from image collections, such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), are capable of generating high-quality novel views by leveraging graphics primitives with view-dependent appearance. In 3DGS, spherical harmonic (SH) are employed to model view-dependent color, resulting in a large number of SH coefficients per primitive and large memory requirements. While compression approaches have been proposed to mitigate this problem, they do not exploit the capabilities of modern Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) for parallel decoding and rendering. In this paper, we propose a method for compressing SH color coefficients using texture compression schemes specifically designed for efficient parallel GPU decoding and supported by dedicated hardware acceleration. It is shown that those methods can compress color coefficients more effectively than 2D textures by exploiting the fact that primitives can be locally grouped and reordered according to color. Furthermore, we introduce a bit-rate control strategy that preserves random access, enabling large-scale parallelization without compromising rendering performance. Experimental results using BC1 and BC7 texture compression formats show that GPU-based decompression can be achieved with negligible or imperceptible degradation in the visual quality of rendered 3DGS scenes.

</details>


### [105] [VTM-Nav: Hierarchical Visual-Topological Memory for Cross-Episode Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14514)
*Xiaoran Xu,Yupeng Wu,Tianyu Xue,Yifan Xu,Xuanran Dong,Xiaoshan Yang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨回合目标导航任务，并设计了一种基于VLM的免训练框架VTN，通过持久化视觉-拓扑记忆（VTM）在回合间复用场景经验，在多个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标导航方法通常采用回合制协议，每次结束后重置场景状态，忽略了在同一场景中跨回合复用经验的可能性，限制了智能体在实际场景中的持续学习能力。

Method: 提出VTN框架，核心是持久化层次化视觉-拓扑记忆（VTM），在房间级和物体级组织场景知识，通过粗到细匹配检索相关经验；仅在记忆与当前观测一致时提供软引导；并设有保守执行防护机制减少振荡、阻塞和过早停止。

Result: 在HM3D v0.1、HM3D v0.2和MP3D三个基准上均取得最佳性能，超过了增强版WMNav基线，验证了结构化视觉-拓扑经验复用的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨回合经验复用能显著提升目标导航性能，VTN中VTM提供的结构化记忆引导是有效的关键因素。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation requires an embodied agent to locate and reach an instance of a specified object category in an indoor environment. Recent training-free approaches leverage vision-language models (VLMs) for open-vocabulary semantic reasoning, but are typically evaluated under an episodic protocol that resets all scene-specific state after each episode. We introduce Cross-Episode Object-Goal Navigation, in which an agent repeatedly operates in the same scene, retains only self-acquired experience, and keeps its model parameters fixed. To support experience reuse, we present \method, a training-free VLM navigation framework with a persistent hierarchical Visual-Topological Memory (VTM). The VTM organizes scene knowledge at room and object levels and retrieves relevant experience through coarse-to-fine matching, providing memory as soft guidance only when it agrees with current observations. A conservative execution guard further mitigates oscillations, blocked motions, and premature stopping. Under a controlled same-scene protocol, we evaluate \method{} on three benchmarks, HM3D v0.1, HM3D v0.2, and MP3D, and compare it with a strengthened WMNav baseline augmented with cross-episode textual memory, while keeping the VLM backbone and action pipeline identical. \method{} achieves the best performance across all three benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness and robustness of structured visual-topological experience reuse across datasets.

</details>


### [106] [Uni-AdaVD: Universal Concept Erasure for Visual Generation via Orthogonal Value Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14521)
*Qifan Zhou,Yuan Wang,Yanbin Hao,Xiang Wang,Kuien Liu,Richang Hong,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Uni-AdaVD 提出了一种通用的推理时概念擦除框架，通过将多模态注意力值空间作为统一干预空间，结合编码器感知的目标表示构建和正交值分解自适应擦除偏移，在不更新模型权重的情况下实现视觉生成模型中的单/多概念擦除，适用于U-Net、DiT、自回归图像生成器和文本到视频模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成模型从无筛选的预训练数据中不可避免地学习到不良概念，现有的概念擦除方法通常依赖特定架构，难以在移除目标概念的同时保留非目标内容和生成先验。

Method: 1) 将多模态注意力的值空间作为统一干预空间；2) 编码器感知的目标表示构建，以在不同异构文本编码器中定位目标语义；3) 正交值分解结合自适应擦除偏移，抑制目标语义方向，无需更新原始模型权重。

Result: 在U-Net、DiT、自回归图像生成器以及文本到视频模型上进行了广泛实验，在单概念和多概念擦除任务上均表现强劲，同时保留了非目标先验。

Conclusion: Uni-AdaVD为现代视觉生成模型提供了一种高效且可适应的安全机制，代码已开源。
