Method: 采用固定不相交群体和add-or-remove-one邻接关系，将隐私预算定义为依赖于受影响计数的非增函数 $v(n)$，并约束所有阶Rényi散度的两个方向。修正原始严格相对误差条件，引入边界容忍度。上界通过直接特殊化已有的移位变换框架（shifted-log Gaussian mechanism）实现；下界通过信息论论证证明逆平方率最优。

Result: Shifted-log Gaussian机制可实现 $v(n)=O_r(n^{-2})$；对任意 $0<r<1$，任何满足正计数效用要求和count-dependent zCDP的机制必须满足 $v(n)=\Omega_r(n^{-2})$，即逆平方率最优。在大计数-小误差极限下，前置系数介于 $\pi/(4e^2)$ 和 $1/\pi$ 之间，因子小于3。当 $r=1$ 时，数据无关发布可在零隐私损失下满足修正后的判据。

Conclusion: 逆平方率是计数依赖型zCDP直方图问题下的最优隐私预算衰减速率；修正后的相对误差条件是合理的，且系数界紧密到因子3以内。

Abstract: Pujol and Desfontaines asked whether a private histogram can allow more error on larger counts and use that slack to protect members of larger groups more strongly. We study this question for fixed disjoint groups under add-or-remove-one adjacency. The privacy budget $v(n)$ depends on the affected count, is nonincreasing, and must bound both Rényi-divergence directions at every order. This is the count-dependent form of zero-concentrated differential privacy (zCDP) studied here. The original strict relative-error condition is impossible at count zero. We therefore make the boundary tolerance explicit by requiring $\mathbb{E}\lvert\widehat{x}_i-x_i\rvert < r\max\{x_i,1\}$, without changing the requirement at any positive count. Our main result determines the best dependence on group size. For the upper bound, we directly specialize an existing shifted-transformation framework. The resulting shifted-log Gaussian mechanism has a certified budget $v(n)=O_r(n^{-2})$. Conversely, for every fixed $0<r<1$, any mechanism satisfying the same positive-count utility requirement and count-dependent zCDP must have $v(n)=Ω_r(n^{-2})$. Thus the inverse-square rate is optimal under the repaired formulation. A many-count information argument further places the leading coefficient in the large-count-then-small-error limit between $π/(4e^2)$ and $1/π$, a factor below three. At $r=1$, a data-independent release meets the repaired criterion with zero privacy loss.

</details>


### [178] [A Measurement Study of AI-Environment Realism Gaps in Malware-Analysis Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14434)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Babangida Bappah,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统测量了AI环境工件作为沙箱逃逸的新攻击面，提出了AIprint探测框架，从284个开源AI项目中提取450个独特工件，在7个沙箱后端和3个AI参考主机上评估，发现12个工件在参考主机上出现而沙箱中缺失，并通过安装实验和自适应欺骗实验揭示了操作不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境感知型恶意软件不断进化，传统沙箱逃逸方法（虚拟化伪影、时序差异等）已难以应对；AI软件生态系统留下的持久性工件未被系统性研究，存在新的沙箱逃逸攻击面。

Method: 提出AIprint探测框架：从284个开源AI项目系统性提取450个独特工件，编译为无权限Windows探测程序；在7个商业/开源沙箱后端和3个AI参考主机上评估；进行214步因果关系安装实验和自适应欺骗实验。

Result: 传统VM检测基线无法可靠区分真实AI系统与沙箱；12个AI环境工件在所有参考主机上出现而在所有沙箱后端上均缺失；安装实验证实AI工具安装与工件积累的因果关系；欺骗实验揭示再现真实AI环境成本远高于检测浅层欺骗。

Conclusion: AI环境工件构成了新的沙箱逃逸攻击面，沙箱需要增强对AI环境的模拟能力；欺骗实验揭示的操作不对称性表明简单伪造容易被检测，沙箱需投入更高成本才能有效模拟真实AI环境。

Abstract: Sandboxing remains a core technique for observing suspicious program behavior, yet environment-aware malware increasingly suppresses execution when analysis is suspected. Prior generations of sandbox evasion focused on virtualization artifacts, timing discrepancies, and wear-and-tear realism. In this paper, we present the first systematic measurement study of AI-environment artifacts as a new sandbox-evasion surface. We operationalize this realism gap through AIprint, a probe framework that captures persistent artifacts left behind by AI-capable software ecosystems, including AI-assistant configuration directories, model caches, environment variables, local inference services, and package dependencies.
  We systematically extract 450 unique artifacts from 284 open-source AI projects on GitHub, compile them into unprivileged Windows probes, and evaluate them across seven commercial and open-source sandbox backends together with three AI-capable reference hosts. Our results show that traditional VM-detection baselines fail to reliably distinguish real AI-capable systems from modern sandboxes, whereas twelve AI-environment artifacts appear on the reference hosts and on none of the evaluated backends. A controlled 214-step installation experiment establishes a causal relationship between AI tool and package installation and measurable AI-environment artifact accumulation, while adaptive spoofing experiments reveal a fundamental operational asymmetry: reproducing convincing AI software environments is substantially more expensive than detecting shallow spoofing.

</details>


### [179] [Disclosure Divergence: Measuring Privacy Policy and Data Safety Misalignment at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14442)
*Mst Eshita Khatun,Lamine Noureddine,Sideeq Bello,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对6,051个Android应用的隐私政策与Google Play数据安全标签之间的一致性进行了大规模实证研究，发现两者在数据披露上存在显著不一致，尤其是在敏感数据类型和共享操作方面，并提出了敏感性加权风险评分来量化隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用增长迅速，用户数据隐私日益受到关注。隐私政策和平台数据安全标签作为两种独立的披露渠道，可能呈现不一致的应用数据实践描述，给用户和监管机构带来不确定性。目前缺乏对这两种披露机制之间一致性的系统性大规模实证研究。

Method: 对6,051个Android应用进行大规模实证研究，使用基于LLM的提取框架和统一的模式（涵盖14个Google Play数据类别及收集和共享两种操作），逐应用和逐类别测量一致性，并引入敏感性加权风险评分来强调高风险数据类型。

Result: 发现披露不一致在敏感类别（如个人信息和设备标识符）中尤为突出；共享披露的一致性低于收集披露；高隐私风险集中在与持续监控和通信相关的应用类别中。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了当前披露机制的结构性缺陷，强调了在平台级隐私报告中需要更强的验证和更高的透明度。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of mobile applications, user data privacy has become an increasing concern. While privacy policies describe how apps collect and share data, platforms such as Google Play provide Data Safety labels intended to summarize these practices. Because these disclosure channels are declared separately, they may present inconsistent representations of app data practices, creating uncertainty for users and regulators. In this work, we conducted a large-scale empirical study of disclosure consistency across 6,051 Android apps. Using an LLM-based extraction framework and a unified schema over 14 Google Play data categories and two operations (collection and sharing), we measure per-app and per-category consistency and introduce a sensitivity-weighted risk score that emphasizes high-risk data types. We find that misalignment disproportionately affects sensitive categories such as personal information and device identifiers, with sharing disclosures exhibiting lower consistency than collection disclosures. Elevated privac risk is concentrated in app categories associated with persistent monitoring and communication. Overall, our findings highlight structural gaps in current disclosure mechanisms and underscore the need for stronger verification and greater transparency in platform-level privacy reporting.

</details>


### [180] [Context Contamination in LLM Analysis of Network Security Logs: Poison with Passive Prompt Injection and Mitigation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14493)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Hemanth Hegadehalli Madhavarao,Lei Xu,Weidong Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LogInject框架，系统性地评估了LLM日志分析系统中的被动提示注入攻击。通过构建包含12,847条日志的基准测试集，在三个生产级LLM上实验发现基线攻击成功率高达88.2%（平均83.4%），并提出Context Stitching技术将载荷分片跨日志条目实现76.4%成功率。结合输入过滤、提示加固和输出验证的防御可将攻击降低90.4%，但仍有8.4%残存漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在安全运营中心日志分析中的广泛部署引入了新的安全风险：日志中嵌入的对抗性内容可能在分析师查询时被LLM执行，形成被动提示注入攻击。现有研究缺乏对这一架构性漏洞的系统评估框架。

Method: 构建LogInject-1.0基准测试（2,569条对抗样本），定义四种攻击目标（活动隐藏、误报生成、信息窃取、输出劫持），对三个生产LLM进行攻击实验。提出Context Stitching技术用于跨日志条目分片注入。评估分层防御组合效果。

Result: 基线攻击成功率最高88.2%（平均83.4%）；Context Stitching技术成功率76.4%；分层防御可降低90.4%攻击，但仍有8.4%残存漏洞。

Conclusion: LLM日志分析系统存在固有的混乱代理漏洞（confused deputy vulnerability），不可信数据与可信指令在模型注意力中无法区分，需要纵深防御架构和持续人工监督。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in Security Operations Centers for log analysis tasks including summarization, alert triage, and threat investigation. These systems ingest logs from external-facing services and process network logs as natural language contexts to generate security insights. We demonstrate that this architectural pattern introduces a critical vulnerability: adversaries can embed prompt injection payloads in log-generating fields that persist in storage and are executed when analysts query the LLM, achieving what we term passive prompt injection. We present LogInject, a systematic framework for evaluating these threats. Using LogInject-1.0, a benchmark of 12,847 log entries including 2,569 adversarial samples, we evaluate three production LLMs across four attack objectives: activity concealment, false positive generation, information exfiltration, and output hijacking. Our findings reveal an up to 88.2% attack success rate (83.4% average across models) under the baseline conditions. We introduce Context Stitching, a novel technique that fragments payloads across multiple log entries to evade stateless filters while exploiting LLM long-context reasoning, achieving a 76.4% success rate. As mitigation, we evaluate layered defenses by combining input filtering, prompt hardening, and output validation, demonstrating a 90.4% attack reduction, although 8.4% residual vulnerability persists. Our results establish that LLM-based log analysis creates an inherent confused deputy vulnerability where untrusted data and trusted instructions compete indistinguishably for model attention, requiring defense in-depth architectures and continued human oversight for security-critical decisions.

</details>


### [181] [Fully Automated End-to-End Adversary Emulation from MITRE ATT\&CK Based Cyber Threat Intelligence Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14566)
*Jueon Choi,Seojun Lee,Sanggwon Yun,Kwanghoon Choi,Gunjin Cha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全自动化的端到端框架，利用大语言模型从MITRE ATT&CK对齐的CTI报告中生成、执行和修订Caldera攻击剧本，并通过故障类型感知恢复机制持续提升执行成功率，在Claude Sonnet 4.5上达到最优性能（84.22%执行成功率），且在所有评估模型上故障恢复机制一致提升14.59%p至17.23%p，在AURORA数据集上超越现有最先进的对手仿真系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手仿真工作要么执行预编写的剧本，要么部分自动化剧本生成，缺乏从CTI报告到执行再到故障恢复的完全自动化统一流程。尤其是最先进的AURORA系统仍需部分人工干预，且无法根据执行失败情况修订剧本。

Method: 提出全自动端到端框架，统一了Caldera剧本生成（从CTI报告自动生成）、自动执行以及故障类型感知恢复机制三个环节。故障恢复机制能够识别执行失败类型并有针对性地修订失败的能力（Abilities）。

Result: 在11个CTI报告上使用Claude Sonnet 4.5、GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Pro和Grok 4 Fast评估，Claude Sonnet 4.5取得最佳：每个剧本27.3个Abilities，修订后执行成功率84.22%，CTI精确率73.95%、召回率52.48%、F1值60.50%。故障恢复机制在所有模型上一致提升执行成功率14.59%p至17.23%p。在AURORA数据集的10个CTI报告上，最终执行成功率超越AURORA。

Conclusion: 提出的自动化框架有效实现了从CTI报告到对手仿真的全流程自动化，故障类型感知恢复机制是提升执行成功率的关键组件，在多个LLM模型上均表现出鲁棒性，整体性能超越了当前最先进的对手仿真系统AURORA。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully automated end-to-end framework for adversary emulation from MITRE ATT&CK-aligned CTI reports using LLMs. Unlike prior work, which either executes prewritten playbooks or partially automates playbook generation, our framework unifies playbook generation, execution, and failure recovery in a single workflow. In particular, although AURORA, the most recent prior study, generates playbooks from CTI reports, it still requires partial manual intervention and does not revise playbooks based on execution failures. Our framework generates Caldera playbooks from CTI reports, executes them automatically, and revises failed Abilities through a failure-type-aware recovery mechanism. Evaluated on 11 CTI reports with Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and Grok 4 Fast, the framework achieved its best results with Claude Sonnet 4.5: 27.3 Abilities per playbook, 84.22% execution success after revision, and CTI Precision, Recall, and F1 of 73.95%, 52.48%, and 60.50%, respectively. The failure recovery mechanism consistently improved execution success across all evaluated LLM models by 14.59%p to 17.23%p. On the 10 CTI reports selected from AURORA's dataset, this mechanism further increased the final execution success rate, surpassing that of AURORA, which represents the state-of-the-art adversary emulation system.

</details>


### [182] [Qubes OS Security in the Public Record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14587)
*Alfonso De Gregorio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Qubes OS的109份安全公告（2011-2025）进行了协议驱动的纵向分析，发现79.8%的漏洞可归因于上游组件（Xen、CPU/微架构等），而非Qubes核心逻辑；披露活动在2015年后趋于稳定但维持在较高水平，上游依赖性是主要安全负担来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Qubes OS的架构使组件边界具有安全相关性，但缺乏对其安全公告记录的纵向测量研究。本文旨在通过系统性分析方法评估公开咨询记录的特征，揭示上游依赖对Qubes安全态势的影响。

Method: 采用协议驱动的纵向分析方法，涵盖109份Qubes安全公告（QSB）、464份Xen安全公告（XSA）追踪器及二次漏洞事件敏感性序列。方法包括：确定性组件归因审计、变点分析、过度离散检验、严重性代理加权、删失敏感性分析、文档延迟下界估计，以及基线感知的漏洞发现模型（VDM）评估。

Result: 官方追踪器中464份XSA有113份影响Qubes；109份QSB中87份（79.8%）归因于上游组件；变点分析确定2015年Q1为季度公告序列的主要断点；2018年后年度披露率统计上呈平坦趋势；泊松推断在离散诊断和负二项敏感性检验中保持稳定；归因编码手册在分层30-QSB审计中表现良好；S形VDM拟合具有描述性但短期预测未显著优于滚动均值基线。

Conclusion: Qubes公开咨询记录总体稳定但并非平静：披露活动在早期水平之上达到平台期，而安全负担持续集中在上游信任锚点（Xen、CPU/微架构等）。

Abstract: Qubes OS is a revealing case for security measurement because its architecture makes component boundaries security-relevant. We present a protocol-driven longitudinal analysis of 109 public Qubes Security Bulletins (QSBs, 2011--2025), the official Qubes-maintained Xen Security Advisory (XSA) tracker, and a secondary vulnerability-event sensitivity series. The study measures the public advisory record rather than latent vulnerability incidence or realized compromise. The methodology combines audited deterministic component attribution, change-point analysis, overdispersion checks, severity-proxy weighting, censoring sensitivity, documentary latency lower bounds, and baseline-aware evaluation of vulnerability discovery models (VDMs).
  The results show persistent upstream dependence in that public record. On the official tracker, 113 of 464 XSAs affect Qubes; under primary labeling, 87 of 109 QSBs (79.8\%) are attributable to Xen, CPU/microarchitectural, or other upstream components rather than Qubes-core logic, with similar results under weighted views. Change-point analyses identify 2015Q1 as the dominant break in the quarterly advisory series, while post-2018 annual disclosure rates are statistically flat. Poisson inferences are stable under dispersion diagnostics and negative-binomial sensitivity checks. The attribution codebook performs well in a stratified 30-QSB audit, and S-shaped VDMs fit descriptively but do not significantly outperform a rolling-mean baseline in short-horizon forecasts.
  Overall, the Qubes public advisory record appears stable, but not quiet: disclosure activity plateaus at a higher level than in the earliest years, while the observed burden remains concentrated in upstream trust anchors.

</details>


### [183] [Bad Memory: Evaluating Prompt Injection Risks from Memory in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14611)
*Soham Gadgil,David Alexander,Sai Sunku,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究了基于记忆的智能体系统中的提示注入攻击，发现虽然难以让智能体用不可信的外部内容覆盖自身记忆文件，但已植入记忆中的载荷能成功攻击当前和未来会话，且攻击成功率和持久性因系统、模型、对抗目标和多会话攻击序列而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统通过记忆文件、行为偏好和知识库维持跨会话的持久状态，这虽然提升了有用性和自我改进能力，但也为提示注入创造了新的攻击面——恶意指令可嵌入持久文件并影响未来行为。现有研究缺乏对这一新威胁面的系统分析。

Method: 在沙盒化合成工作区中，评估两个智能体系统（Anthropic Claude Code 和 OpenAI Codex）及四个模型（Claude Haiku 4.5、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.5），系统性地测试提示注入攻击在记忆型智能体系统中的表现。

Result: 1) 用不可信外部内容让智能体覆盖自身记忆文件具有难度；2) 已植入记忆文件中的载荷能成功攻击当前和未来会话；3) 攻击成功率和持久性在不同系统、模型、对抗目标和多会话攻击序列间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 持久性记忆改变了提示注入的威胁模型，亟需设计既能保护记忆更新又能保留有用智能体适应能力的防御机制。

Abstract: A growing class of agentic systems maintain persistent state across sessions through memory files, behavioral preferences, and knowledge bases. While this makes agents more useful and self-improving, it also creates a new attack surface for prompt injections in which malicious instructions can be embedded within persistent files and influence future behavior. In this work, we study prompt injection attacks in memory-based agentic systems using a sandboxed synthetic workspace. We evaluate two agentic systems, Anthropic Claude Code and OpenAI Codex, across four models: Claude Haiku 4.5, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5.2, and GPT-5.5. Our results show that although it is difficult to make an agent overwrite its own memory files using untrusted external content, payloads already planted in those files can successfully attack current and future sessions. Attack success and payload persistence vary substantially across systems, models, adversarial goals, and multi-session attack sequences. These findings show that persistent memory changes the threat model for prompt injection and motivate defenses that protect memory updates without removing useful agent adaptation.

</details>


### [184] [MemPoison: Uncovering Persistent Memory Threats and Structural Blind Spots in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14651)
*Jifeng Gao,Kang Xia,Yi Zhang,Xiaobin Hong,Mingkai Lin,Xingshen Wei,Wenzhong Li,Sanglu Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemPoison是一个针对持久性外部记忆安全漏洞的基准测试与分析框架，包含1227个手工验证案例，覆盖4种攻击类型、3种注入渠道和3种记忆基质，在10个模型家族上评估。研究发现写时防御（如一致性检查）能抑制直接攻击（L1），但无法有效应对组合攻击（L2）和上下文触发攻击（L3），通过机制影响分解揭示了防御的结构性盲点，主张从静态过滤转向自适应上下文敏感的记忆防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性外部记忆虽然增强了智能体的连续性，但引入了持久性安全漏洞——敌对内容可通过标准交互渠道注入、跨轮次保留并扭曲后续行为，现有防御手段不足以应对这一挑战。

Method: 构建MemPoison基准测试框架（1227个手工验证案例），提出三级攻击分类体系（L1直接单记录破坏、L2组合多记录破坏、L3上下文触发潜伏性破坏），采用机制影响分解（MID）分析防御盲点，在7个开源和3个闭源模型家族上进行评估。

Result: 写时防御（如一致性检查）能有效抑制L1直接攻击，但无法可靠抑制L2组合攻击和L3上下文触发攻击；通过MID分析揭示了写时防御存在结构性盲点，允许看似良性的记录通过联合检索组合或触发条件激活后产生危害。

Conclusion: 应从静态过滤转向自适应、上下文敏感的记忆防御策略，以应对持久性外部记忆中的复合型安全威胁。

Abstract: Persistent external memory enhances agent continuity but introduces persistent security vulnerabilities: adversarial content can be injected via standard interaction channels, retained across turns, and later distort downstream behavior. To address this challenge, we propose MemPoison, a comprehensive benchmark and analysis framework featuring 1227 hand-validated cases across four attack types, three injection channels, and three representative memory substrates, evaluated on seven open-weight and three closed-weight model families. We introduce a three-tier taxonomy: (L1) direct single-record corruption, (L2) compositional multi-record corruption and (L3) context-triggered dormant corruption. Our evaluations reveal a distinct defense frontier: while baseline write-time defenses, such as consistency checks, substantially suppress direct L1 attacks, they fail to reliably suppress L2 and L3 attacks. Through mechanistic influence decomposition (MID), we demonstrate structural blind spots in write-time defenses, which admit seemingly benign records that later become harmful through joint retrieval composition or trigger-conditioned activation. Our findings advocate for shifting from static filtering to adaptive, context-sensitive memory defense strategies.

</details>


### [185] [Lattice-based extended withdrawability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14690)
*Ramses Fernandez-Valencia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于格的后量子签名方案，将Liu等人提出的可扩展可撤销签名扩展为基于Fiat-Shamir with aborts范式的格上构造，实现了可申领环签名：签名者匿名性由黑盒使用的一对多签名保证，确认机制通过绑定签名和隐藏索引承诺实现，无需泄露任何签名者相关信息。在量子随机预言机模型下，基于MLWE、MSIS等困难问题给出了完整的安全性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有可扩展可撤销签名方案在实现时会将签名者相关偏移量明文传输，导致签名者身份泄露。为克服这一缺陷，需要在格上实现真正保护签名者隐私的构造。

Method: 将可扩展可撤销签名实现为可申领环签名：利用一对多签名（one-out-of-N）作为黑盒提供匿名性；通过绑定签名与隐藏索引承诺的揭示实现签名的确权声明；基于Fiat-Shamir with aborts范式构造，基础签名采用无提示、全t的类Dilithium方案，一对多证明层使用已有的格上一对多证明。

Result: 给出完整的正确性、可扩展可撤销性（匿名直到申领）、内部腐败下的不可伪造性以及可申领合理性证明，归约到判定性MLWE、MSIS、一对多方案的匿名性以及基础签名的EUF-CMA安全性，安全性在（量子）随机预言机模型下成立。

Conclusion: 成功构造了格上不泄露签名者信息的可扩展可撤销签名方案，所有安全属性均有严格归约证明，可抵御量子攻击。

Abstract: We extend the extended withdrawable signatures of Liu, Susilo and Baek to lattice-based constructions built on the Fiat-Shamir with aborts paradigm. Departing from an earlier draft that transported a per-signer shift in the clear, which leaks the signer, we realise extended withdrawable signatures as a claimable ring signature: signer ambiguity is provided by a one-out-of-N signature used as a black box (anonymity under full key exposure), and confirmation is the signer's claim, a binding signature together with the opening of a hiding index commitment bound into the transcript. No signer-derived value is published in the clear. We give complete proofs of correctness, extended withdrawability (as anonymity-until-claim), unforgeability under insider corruption, and claimability soundness, reducing to decisional MLWE (commitment hiding), MSIS (commitment binding), the anonymity of the one-out-of-$N$ scheme, and the EUF-CMA security of the base signature, in the (quantum) random-oracle model. We instantiate the base signature with a no-hint, full-$t$ Dilithium-style scheme and the one-out-of-$N$ layer with an established lattice one-out-of-many proof.

</details>


### [186] [FlowGuard: From Signals to Evidence for MCP Security Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14754)
*Baichao An,Pei Chen,Geng Hong,Yueyue Chen,Mengying Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlowGuard是一个基于证据的MCP安全检测系统，通过运行时证据验证执行相关风险并检测语义风险，在1880个MCP用例上实现高F1分数（命令注入0.879，文件系统访问0.942），相比现有动态扫描器延迟降低2.23倍，并在真实环境中从326个服务器报告523个发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP安全扫描器主要基于可疑语义信号进行推理，而非真实执行行为，导致风险评估不可靠（例如凭据字符串可能是占位符而非实际泄露）。需要运行时证据来验证执行相关风险，并对元数据或返回内容中的风险进行细致的语义分析。

Method: FlowGuard结合语义风险分类、重定向引导的载荷缩减、模式验证的探测生成、证据裁决和历史引导的细化五个模块。通过运行时证据验证执行相关风险，同时检测工具元数据和返回内容中的语义风险。

Result: 在包含1880个MCP用例（覆盖五类漏洞）的可执行基准上，FlowGuard在命令注入类别上F1得分为0.879，在文件系统访问类别上为0.942。相比现有动态扫描器，端到端延迟降低2.23倍。真实世界评估中，从326个服务器报告了523个发现。

Conclusion: 基于证据的检测方法能够有效评估MCP交互中的执行相关风险和语义风险，相比纯语义分析方法更可靠。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables LLM agents to interact with external tools through metadata exchange, tool invocation, and response consumption. Existing MCP security scanners primarily reason about suspicious semantic signals rather than real execution behaviors, which can lead to unreliable risk assessment. For example, credential-like strings may simply be placeholders rather than actual leakage. This gap requires runtime evidence for execution-related risks and careful semantic analysis for risks carried in metadata or returned content. We present FlowGuard, an evidence-grounded MCP security detection system. FlowGuard combines semantic risk triage, recon-guided payload narrowing, schema-valid probe generation, evidence adjudication, and history-guided refinement. It verifies execution-related risks through runtime evidence and detects semantic risks in tool metadata and returned content. We evaluate FlowGuard on an executable benchmark containing 1,880 MCP cases across five vulnerability categories. FlowGuard achieves F1 scores of 0.879 and 0.942 on the execution-related Command Injection and File System Access categories, respectively. Compared with existing dynamic scanners, FlowGuard reduces end-to-end latency by up to 2.23x. In the real-world evaluation, FlowGuard reports 523 findings across 326 servers. These results show that evidence-grounded detection can assess both execution-related and semantic risks in MCP interactions.

</details>


### [187] [Is External Database Protection Static in Retrieval-Augmented Generation? Rethinking Privacy Preservation under Dynamic Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14811)
*Gang Zhang,Mingyu Tian,Xukun Luan,Yuanchi Ma,Jinyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PA-HDP框架，通过查询感知的动态分层差分隐私机制，在检索增强生成中根据用户查询动态评估文档隐私风险，实现差异化保护，在减少隐私泄露的同时保持高质量的检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG隐私保护方法基于文档级静态风险假设，将检索文档视为具有相同隐私泄露风险，忽视了RAG中隐私风险高度依赖用户查询这一根本特性，即隐私泄露本质上是查询驱动和动态的。

Method: PA-HDP框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 查询感知风险分层（Query-aware Risk Hierarchy），根据不同查询动态评估文档的隐私风险等级；(2) 自适应敏感实体替换与基于指数机制的文本选择，对高风险内容提供差异化隐私保护，同时保留语义效用。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，PA-HDP在显著降低隐私泄露的同时保持了较高的检索质量，相比现有方法实现了更好的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 通过仅保护在给定查询下真正敏感的内容，PA-HDP最小化了对检索语料库的不必要修改，验证了查询感知的动态差分隐私机制在RAG隐私保护中的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enhances large language models via external document retrieval, but retrieved contexts may leak sensitive information. Current privacy protection methods typically rely on a document-level static risk assumption, treating all retrieved documents as having the same privacy leakage risk. However, this assumption overlooks a fundamental characteristic of RAG: the privacy risk of a document is highly dependent on the user's query, making privacy leakage inherently query-driven and dynamic. To address this challenge, we propose a Prompt-Aware Dynamic Hierarchical Differential Privacy framework (PA-HDP) for privacy-preserving RAG. PA-HDP first performs a prompt-aware risk hierarchy to dynamically assess privacy risks under different queries. It then applies adaptive sensitive entity replacement and exponential mechanism-based text selection to provide differentiated privacy protection while preserving semantic utility. By protecting only the content that is truly sensitive under a given query, PA-HDP minimizes unnecessary modifications to the retrieval corpus. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that PA-HDP significantly reduces privacy leakage while maintaining high retrieval quality, achieving a better privacy-utility trade-off than prior methods.

</details>


### [188] [The Distributed Open-Source Vulnerability Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14900)
*Peter Mandl,Paul Mandl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个概念框架，将漏洞管理描述为信息交换和转换的分布式过程，并识别出导致不同漏洞扫描器结果不一致的四大原因：异构信息源、身份与版本模型的差异、时间变化以及上下文依赖的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管漏洞信息基于共享标准，但不同扫描器对相同的软件清单会报告不一致的结果，这种差异源于开源漏洞生态系统多个阶段而非单一因素，亟需系统性的分析框架。

Method: 本文构建了一个概念框架，追踪漏洞信息从创建、标准化、丰富到上下文依赖解释的完整链路，系统性地分析各环节中可能导致结果差异的因素。

Result: 识别出异构信息源、身份与版本模型差异、时间变化、上下文依赖评估这四类主要原因导致扫描器结果不一致。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于指导分析结果解读、设计可复现的评估方法以及管理动态漏洞知识。

Abstract: Identifying known software vulnerabilities is a central task in software supply chain security management. Although publicly available vulnerability information is based on shared standards, different vulnerability scanners often report divergent results for identical software inventories. These differences do not arise solely from individual data sources or scanner implementations. They can emerge at several stages of the open-source vulnerability ecosystem. This paper presents a conceptual framework that describes vulnerability management as a distributed process of information exchange and transformation. It traces vulnerability information from its creation and standardization through enrichment to context-dependent interpretation. The analysis identifies heterogeneous information sources, divergent identity and version models, temporal change, and context-dependent assessment as major causes of inconsistent scanner findings. It then discusses the implications for interpreting analysis results, designing reproducible evaluation methods, and handling dynamic vulnerability knowledge in practice.

</details>


### [189] [A Queueing-Stability Criterion for Causal IPD-QIM Network Flow Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14954)
*Jiuxiang Cai,Guang Cheng,Guangjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对因果QIM网络流水印的稳定嵌入条件进行理论分析，将嵌入器建模为反射驻留队列，推导出稳定判据：在独立同分布背景流量下，缓冲区稳定当且仅当μ_d > Δ/4（即Δ < 4μ_d）；在平稳遍历和有限状态马尔可夫调制流量下，当时间平均强度ρ̄ < 1时稳定。结合解码下限Δ ≥ cσ_ξ，得到操作窗口Δ ∈ [cσ_ξ, 4μ̄_d)。仿真和真实IPD轨迹验证了该判据的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果QIM水印在突发流量下的稳定嵌入问题主要依赖经验配置，缺乏理论分析。本文旨在建立可测试的稳定可嵌入性判据，为量化步长配置提供理论基线。

Method: 将嵌入器建模为反射驻留队列（reflected dwell queue），采用固定双格点等概率比特规则，其中注入是状态依赖的。通过变换Y_i=δ_i-r_i得到代数Lindley形式恒等式，分析大驻留量下的忙态漂移。

Result: 推导出稳定判据：i.i.d.背景流量下μ_d > Δ/4；马尔可夫调制流量下ρ̄ < 1。结合解码下限得到操作窗口Δ ∈ [cσ_ξ, 4μ̄_d)。仿真和真实流量实验验证了判据的准确性。

Conclusion: 给出了因果QIM网络流水印的可测试稳定嵌入判据和量化步长配置基线，实验证明判据在流局部相关性和突发性下能给出正确的稳定方向。

Abstract: On multi-hop encrypted links such as Tor and cascaded VPNs, tunneling flattens packet lengths and protocol fields, leaving inter-packet delay (IPD) as the main carrier for active flow attribution. Causality lets the embedder delay packets but never advance them, so each quantization-index-modulation (QIM) alignment injects nonnegative dwell into a delay buffer; unbounded dwell breaks lattice alignment and delays the host connection unacceptably. Whether a causal QIM watermark embeds stably on bursty traffic has largely been left to empirical configuration rather than analysis. We model the embedder as a reflected dwell queue under the fixed dual-lattice, equiprobable-bit rule, where injection is state-dependent -- set by the current interval and bit -- rather than exogenous. The substitution $Y_i=δ_i-r_i$ gives only an algebraic Lindley-form identity; stability is governed by the busy-state drift at large dwell, where the effective interval collapses to zero and the mean injection becomes $Δ/4$. Away from the critical boundary, the buffer is stable iff $μ_d>Δ/4$ (i.e. $Δ<4μ_d$) for i.i.d. backgrounds, and, under stationary-ergodic and finite-state Markov-modulated traffic with instantaneous overload, iff the time-average intensity $\barρ<1$. With the exogenous decoding floor $Δ\ge cσ_ξ$ ($c=4Q^{-1}(ε/2)$), this yields the operating window $Δ\in[cσ_ξ,4\barμ_d)$. Simulations confirm a sharp transition at $ρ=1$ set only by the mean; on four real IPD traces, with each simulated chain confined to a single flow, the criterion gives the correct stability direction under flow-local correlation and burstiness, while pooled cross-flow means overestimate the margin. These results give a testable stable-embeddability criterion and a quantization-step configuration baseline for causal QIM network flow watermarking.

</details>


### [190] [NFSA: Non-Forward Secure Aggregation with One Server via Two Layer Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15052)
*Yufei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双层秘密共享的联邦学习安全聚合协议，通过结合Shamir秘密共享和基于PRF的2-out-of-2加法秘密共享，消除了用户间直接通信和服务器转发数据的需要；进一步利用KhPRF支持高维数据聚合，并基于中国剩余定理设计新的编码方法降低用户开销，在100用户场景下通信效率提升近100倍，计算开销降低约17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于服务器的安全聚合协议需要服务器转发用户间的敏感数据，导致通信开销增加并引入潜在安全风险。

Method: 提出双层秘密共享协议：结合Shamir秘密共享与基于PRF的2-out-of-2加法秘密共享，消除用户间直接通信；扩展KhPRF支持高维数据聚合，实现单服务器一次性安全聚合；基于中国剩余定理设计编码方法，减少KhPRF调用次数并缓解模型更新扩展问题。

Result: 在100用户场景下，通信效率提升近100倍，计算开销降低约17%；当输入长度为2^18时，用户计算时间可减少51%至75%。

Conclusion: 该方案在辅助节点开销方面显著优于现有方法，同时消除了服务器转发数据的需要，提升了安全性和效率。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training while preserving privacy by keeping data local. However, the risk of sensitive data leakage through model updates necessitates the use of secure aggregation protocols. Existing server-based secure aggregation protocols typically require the server to forward sensitive data shared between users, which increases communication overhead and introduces potential security risks. In this work, we propose a novel secure aggregation protocol based on two-layer secret sharing to address these issues. By combining Shamir's Secret Sharing with 2-out-of-2 additive secret sharing using a Pseudo-Random Function (PRF), our protocol eliminates direct communication between users, thereby removing the need for the server to forward data. We further extend the protocol with Key-homomorphic PRF (KhPRF) to support high-dimensional data aggregation and apply it to FL, enabling one-shot secure aggregation with a single server and no intermediary data forwarding. To reduce user overhead, we design a new encoding method based on the Chinese Remainder Theorem for the almost KhPRF-based mask, reducing the number of KhPRF calls and mitigating the model update expansion issue after masking. Experimental results show that our scheme significantly outperforms existing methods in terms of auxiliary node overhead. For instance, when the number of users is 100, our scheme improves communication efficiency by nearly 100 times and reduces computational overhead by approximately 17\%. Moreover, user computation time can be reduced by 51\% to 75\% when the input length is $2^{18}$.

</details>


### [191] [DataShield: Uncovering Risky Fine-Tuning Data Across LLMs Through Consensus Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15081)
*Zefeng Wu,Weiwei Qi,Jielong Chen,Tianhang Zheng,Di Hong,Chaochao Lu,Liang He,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DataShield框架，通过多安全对齐LLM的共识子空间对齐来识别风险微调样本和片段，通过语义谱分解提取安全/不安全子空间，实现样本级过滤和片段级掩码，相比基线分别降低ASR 14.6%和32.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖单一模型及其分词器计算的均值向量表示安全方向，有效性和可迁移性受限，无法准确识别导致安全退化的微调数据。

Method: 通过多个安全对齐LLM构建联合安全关键语义空间，在该空间内对安全/不安全数据表示进行语义谱分解提取共识安全与不安全子空间，通过测量数据样本或片段与两个子空间的相对对齐程度来评估风险，支持样本级过滤和细粒度片段级掩码。

Result: 样本过滤降低ASR 14.6%，片段掩码降低ASR 32.3%，同时保持下游任务效用，且无需目标模型特定的风险计算。

Conclusion: DataShield通过多模型共识子空间方法提升了安全风险评估的有效性和可迁移性，在过滤和掩码两种范式下均显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on domain-specific datasets has become a standard paradigm for adapting LLMs to specialized applications. However, recent work has shown that even fine-tuning on benign task-specific data can substantially weaken the safety capabilities of LLMs. While existing efforts have made progress in identifying data responsible for safety degradation, they usually rely on a single mean vector computed over a specific model with its tokenizer to represent the safety direction, which limits both the effectiveness and transferability of their risk assessment measures. To address these limitations, we propose DataShield, a data assessment framework that identifies risky fine-tuning samples and response segments through consensus subspace alignment over joint safety-critical semantic spaces derived from multiple safety-aligned LLMs. Within these spaces, DataShield extracts consensus safe and unsafe subspaces using semantic spectral decomposition over safe and unsafe data representations. The risk of a data sample or segment is then estimated by measuring its relative alignment with the unsafe and safe subspaces, enabling both sample-level filtering and fine-grained segment-level masking. Compared with state-of-the-art filtering and masking baselines, DataShield reduces ASR by 14.6\% with sample filtering and 32.3\% with segment masking, while preserving downstream utility and avoiding target-model-specific risk computation.

</details>


### [192] [Automated Template-free Synthesis of Instruction-Centric Leakage Contracts for Black-Box CPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15118)
*Elvira Moreno,Tiziano Marinaro,Ryan Williams,Marco Patrignani,Roberto Guanciale,Hamed Nemati,Marco Guarnieri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化提取CPU指令级泄露合约的方法，并实现了首个无模板工具malcos，可在x86和ARM黑盒CPU上合成精确且可靠的泄露合约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧信道攻击利用CPU微架构行为差异泄露敏感信息，现有泄露合约虽提供了安全编程基础，但由于微架构复杂性，手工提炼合约工作量大，现代CPU缺乏专用泄露合约。

Method: 提出一种以指令为中心的泄露合约提取方法，实现为malcos工具，该工具无需模板，能自动从黑盒CPU中合成泄露合约。

Result: 在x86和ARM CPU上评估显示，malcos合成的合约相对于合成过程中观测到的所有泄露既是精确的又是可靠的。

Conclusion: 从黑盒CPU中学习泄露合约是可行的，为自动化构建CPU安全抽象提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Side-channel attacks pose a significant security threat for modern computing platforms, because they exploit subtle discrepancies in CPU behaviors to leak sensitive information. To model the information leaked by a CPU via microarchitectural side-channels, recent work proposed leakage contracts: an ISA-level security abstraction that provides the foundations for secure CPU programming. Unfortunately, due to the complexity of current microarchitectures, devising a leakage contract for a CPU requires extensive manual effort and thus modern CPUs lack dedicated leakage contracts.
  We present a methodology to extract instruction-centric leakage contracts for major CPU architectures with minimal manual intervention. We implemented this technique in malcos, the first template-free tool that automates the synthesis of leakage contracts for black-box CPUs. We evaluate malcos on x86 and ARM CPUs, and show that the contracts it synthesizes are precise and sound with respect to all leaks observed during synthesis. Our results demonstrate that learning leakage contracts from black-box CPUs is feasible.

</details>


### [193] [Setup Complete, Now You Are Compromised: Weaponizing Setup Instructions Against AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15143)
*Aadesh Bagmar,Pushkar Saraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AI编码代理在设置项目时，会不加验证地读取并安装文档中列出的依赖项，攻击者可通过修改README、requirements文件或Makefile等文档，引导代理安装来自不可信源、含已知漏洞或名称混淆的恶意包，使文档本身成为代码执行向量。本文首次系统评估了通过项目设置文档发起的供应链攻击，针对前沿模型在5类攻击、12个场景中进行测试。研究发现：同一模型在不同代理框架下表现不同——安全取决于框架-模型组合；代理能可靠检测明显拼写错误，但对分隔符混淆名称（如azurecore冒充azure-core）识别率低；基于源的攻击（如注册表重定向）几乎全部失败；安全提示仅能恢复部分差距；确定性预安装检查可基本消除风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注直接恶意包投毒，但忽略了AI编码代理在读取文档后自动安装依赖这一环节的安全漏洞。攻击者只需修改文档中的依赖名称、版本或来源，即可实现供应链攻击，这一攻击面尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建了12个攻击场景（覆盖5类攻击：拼写错误、分隔符混淆、注册表重定向、已知漏洞版本、名称冒充），在多个生产级编码代理框架上对前沿模型进行系统测试，并基于真实安全事件设计攻击样本。

Result: 1) 同一模型在不同代理框架下表现不一致，安全取决于框架-模型组合；2) 明显拼写错误能被检测，但分隔符混淆名称（如azurecore→azure-core）常被忽略；3) 注册表重定向攻击几乎在所有模型和框架上失败；4) npm和Cargo生态中源盲点普遍存在；5) 安全提示只能改善被明确指出的维度；6) 确定性预安装检查能有效弥补大部分漏洞。

Conclusion: AI编码代理的依赖安装环节存在严重安全漏洞，文档成为新的供应链攻击向量。当前模型和代理组合难以有效防御此类攻击，需引入确定性安全检查机制而非依赖模型自身判断。

Abstract: AI coding agents set up projects by reading documentation and installing the dependencies it lists, without verifying their names, sources, or known vulnerabilities. By editing only a README, requirements file, or Makefile, an attacker can redirect the agent to an untrusted registry, a known-vulnerable version, or a wrong-but-plausible name: documentation becomes a vector for code execution. We present the first systematic evaluation of package-install-time supply-chain attacks delivered through ordinary project-setup documentation across production coding-agent harnesses, probing frontier models on twelve scenarios in five attack classes, grounded in documented incidents. The same model catches an attack through one harness and installs it through another: install-time security rests on the harness-model combination, not the model alone. Agents catch blatant typosquats reliably, but plausible separator-confusion names (azurecore for azure-core) slip through, and how often depends on the harness-model pairing. Source-based attacks like registry redirection are missed almost everywhere. The source blind spot recurs on npm and Cargo, where nearly every model installs the untrusted dependency; name detection carries over less consistently across ecosystems. Security-oriented prompts recover part of the gap but only for the dimension they name; a deterministic pre-install check that verifies names, sources, and versions before any code runs closes most of it.

</details>


### [194] [Beyond Success Rate: Cost-Aware Evaluation of Offensive and Defensive Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15263)
*Paul Kassianik,Blaine Nelson,Yaron Singer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出安全智能体的评估不仅应关注峰值攻击能力，更应通过成本-成功率视角评估模型在红队（CTF攻防）和蓝队（SOC安全运维）任务中的表现。研究发现：红队性能随推理计算增加而提升，开源模型可接近闭源前沿系统；蓝队防御任务则更依赖工具使用和遥测导航，而非单纯推理预算。主张安全智能体基准测试应同时衡量经济效率和操作适配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全智能体评估主要关注峰值攻击能力（漏洞发现、渗透测试、CTF完成度等），忽略操作安全中每个推理步骤、工具调用、遥测查询和富化请求都会消耗预算的事实。因此需要从成本-成功率角度重新评估模型在红蓝对抗任务中的实际效能。

Method: 在进攻性Cybench挑战和防御性Splunk BOTS v1调查任务上，通过固定成本水平比较模型表现，并将性能分解为推理成本（inference spend）和工具成本（tool spend）两个维度进行分析。

Result: 进攻性CTF性能随测试时计算增加而提升，开源权重模型可接近前沿闭源系统且保持成本竞争力；防御性SOC调查则无法通过增加推理预算获得同等收益，成功更依赖于工具使用的纪律性、遥测导航能力和选择性富化策略。

Conclusion: 安全智能体基准测试应同时衡量经济效率和操作适配度而非仅任务成功率；成本感知的、原生SOC环境下的评估能更清晰反映模型实际可用性及防御智能体仍需改进的方向。

Abstract: Security-agent evaluations commonly measure peak offensive capability under generous inference budgets, emphasizing vulnerability discovery, exploit development, penetration testing, and CTF completion. Such measurements are useful but incomplete: in operational security, every reasoning step, tool call, telemetry query, and enrichment request consumes budget. We evaluate language-model security agents through this cost-success lens on offensive Cybench challenges and defensive Splunk BOTS v1 investigation challenges. Instead of reporting only best-case success, we compare models at fixed cost levels and decompose performance by inference spend and tool spend. Our results show distinct scalingregimes for red- and blue-team tasks. Offensive CTF performance improves with additional test-time compute, and scaled open-weight models can approach frontier proprietary systems while remaining cost-competitive. Defensive SOC investigation does not scale in the same way: success depends more heavily on disciplined tool use, telemetry navigation, and selective enrichment than on raw reasoning budget alone. We argue that security-agent benchmarks should measure economic efficiency and operational fit alongside task success. Cost-aware, SOC-native evaluations provide a clearer picture of which models are practically useful today and where defensive agents still need to improve. We present an interactive website with our results https://evals.frontier.security.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Generalized Neural Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14122)
*Natan Hilario da Silva,Vicente Garibay Cancho,Adriano Kamimura Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了广义神经分布回归（GNDR）框架，将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布的参数空间，通过两步半参数估计解决深度架构的非可识别性与最大似然理论的矛盾，实现基于Fisher信息矩阵的严谨不确定性量化，并在多种数据模态（过离散临床计数、右删失转录组生存数据、零截断年龄分布）上验证了优越的分布校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在分布回归中面临两个核心挑战：一是深层架构的固有非可识别性（不同参数组合可产生相同似然值）与经典最大似然理论之间的冲突；二是缺乏系统的不确定性量化方法，无法为预测结果提供可靠的置信区间和容忍区间。

Method: 提出GNDR框架：将深度网络作为固定的非线性基展开，隔离终端预测头，采用两步半参数估计流程。第一步训练整个网络，第二步固定上游网络参数，仅对预测头进行似然优化，从而提取解析的Fisher信息矩阵，并利用多元Delta方法生成观测特定的置信带和容忍区间。

Result: 在多个实际应用中验证了框架的通用性和优越分布校准能力：过离散临床计数数据、基于混合治愈框架的右删失转录组生存数据、以及直接从无结构面部图像中提取的零截断年龄分布。

Conclusion: GNDR框架成功弥合了深度学习和经典统计推断之间的鸿沟，为深度学习预测提供了严谨的不确定性量化工具；该方法已开源实现于Python包thetaflow中。

Abstract: We introduce the Generalized Neural Distributional Regression (GNDR) framework, which seamlessly embeds deep neural networks into the parameter space of classical probability distributions. To reconcile the inherent non-identifiability of deep architectures with maximum likelihood theory, we propose a two-step semi-parametric estimation procedure. By isolating the terminal prediction heads and treating the upstream network as a fixed, non-linear basis expansion, GNDR enables the extraction of analytical Fisher Information matrices. This facilitates rigorous uncertainty quantification, generating observation-specific confidence bands and tolerance intervals via the multivariate Delta method. We demonstrate the framework's versatility and superior distributional calibration across diverse data modalities, including overdispersed clinical counts, right-censored transcriptomic survival profiles under a mixture cure framework, and zero-truncated age distributions derived directly from unstructured facial images. The methodology is natively implemented in the open-source Python package \textit{thetaflow}.

</details>


### [196] [Operator-Informed Gaussian Processes for Complex Helmholtz Wavefields: From Synthetic Benchmarks to In Vivo Brain Elastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14193)
*Boyuan Deng,Kshitiz Upadhyay,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将算子条件的高斯过程回归扩展至复值Helmholtz问题，通过将复算子实部化处理为等效耦合实块，在标准实值GP框架下实现推理。该方法在1-3维基准测试中与有限差分和神经网络基线竞争力相当，且所需内部约束点更少，并返回波场后验分布而非点估计。应用于脑磁共振弹性成像时，多尺度先验重建的剪切旋度场与测量值的相关性达到0.77。论文指出校准不确定性是概率波场推理的核心下一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散介质中Helmholtz方程的复值波数使得传统实值算子条件高斯过程方法无法直接应用，而现有方法几乎只处理实值场。论文旨在扩展算子条件GP回归以处理复值Helmholtz问题，实现从稀疏、含噪数据中推理波场并量化不确定性。

Method: 将复Helmholtz算子实部化处理为等价的耦合实块系统，使标准实值GP条件化方法可应用于复值问题。设计从对角先验到多尺度变体的先验族，在PDE残差和边界条件上施加约束。通过1D至3D基准测试与有限差分和神经网络基线对比，并在体内脑磁共振弹性成像数据上验证。

Result: 该方法在1-3维基准测试中与有限差分和神经网络基线竞争力相当，所需内部约束点更少；在脑磁共振弹性成像任务中，多尺度先验重建的剪切旋度场与测量相关性达0.77（超过0.75目标）；发现增益来自多尺度核而非实-虚耦合；识别出由模型失配导致的低频精度上限以及未校准的后验不确定性问题。

Conclusion: 论文成功将算子条件GP回归扩展至复值Helmholtz问题，在特定任务上达到可用精度。但后验不确定性尚未校准，校准不确定性成为耗散介质中概率波场推理的核心下一步研究方向。

Abstract: The Helmholtz equation governs time-harmonic wave propagation, and in dissipative media a complex modulus renders its squared wavenumber $κ^2$ complex. Inferring such fields from sparse, noisy data calls for solvers that also quantify their own uncertainty. Physics-informed Gaussian-process (GP) regression supplies this by returning a posterior over the solution, yet operator-conditioned formulations have been developed almost exclusively for real-valued fields. We extend operator-informed GP regression to complex-valued Helmholtz problems by realifying the complex operator into an equivalent coupled real block, which enables inference with standard real-valued GP conditioning. The construction admits a family of priors, from a proper diagonal prior to coregionalized and multiscale variants, and conditions on PDE residuals and boundary traces. On benchmark problems in one to three dimensions, the solver is competitive with finite-difference and neural-network baselines at a far smaller interior-constraint budget. Unlike those deterministic baselines, it returns a posterior over the complex wavefield rather than a point estimate. Applied to \textit{in vivo} brain magnetic resonance elastography, a proper multiscale prior reconstructs the shear curl field to a correlation of $0.77$ with measurement, above a $0.75$ target. The gain arises from the multiscale kernel rather than from real--imaginary coupling. We further identify a low-frequency accuracy ceiling set by model mismatch and a posterior uncertainty that is not yet calibrated. Calibrated uncertainty therefore emerges as the central next step for probabilistic wavefield inference in dissipative media.

</details>


### [197] [Spectral Concentration and Recovery in Sparse High-Dimensional Random Geometric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14304)
*Manuel Fernandez,Yizhe Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏随机几何图，其中边由高维向量的内积超过阈值决定。主要贡献包括：在连接性尺度上改进了谱范数界；在低维度条件下实现了潜在几何的恢复；首次证明了高斯混合块模型的精确恢复结果。方法结合了正交多项式展开、解耦和矩阵集中不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维随机几何图的谱性质和几何恢复能力，改进现有理论界（如Liu等人2023年的谱范数界和Li & Schramm 2023年的恢复保证），并为同步问题（如Kuramoto模型）和混合模型聚类提供理论基础。

Method: 使用正交多项式展开（如Hermite/Chebyshev多项式）处理边缘依赖结构，结合解耦技术和矩阵集中不等式（如矩阵Bernstein不等式），避免传统的迹矩论证。

Result: 1) 谱范数界：\|A-𝔼A\|=O(√(np log n)+npτ)（高概率），比Liu等人更紧且假设更弱；2) 几何恢复：当np≫log n且d≪np log(1/p)/log n时，潜在向量和相对Gram矩阵误差消失；3) 精确恢复：在最优连接性np=Ω(log n)下，半定规划SDP在中度分离区间精确恢复所有标签，且证明了分离度不足时精确恢复不可能。

Conclusion: 本文在高维稀疏随机几何图的理论分析上取得了显著进展，包括更优的谱范数界、更低的维度需求以及首次的精确恢复结果，为后续研究提供了新的分析框架（正交多项式+解耦+矩阵集中）。

Abstract: We study sparse random geometric graphs generated by connecting pairs of high-dimensional vectors whose inner product exceeds a threshold. The latent vectors are sampled either uniformly from the sphere or from a standard Gaussian distribution. Although every edge appears with probability $p$, the edges are dependent through their shared latent vectors. For the spherical model, at the connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, we prove $\|A-\mathbb E A\|=O\left(\sqrt{np\log n}+npτ\right)$, with high probability, where $τ$ is the cap threshold. This sharpens the spectral norm bound of Liu, Mohanty, Schramm, and Yang (2023) under weaker assumptions. An analogous result holds for the Gaussian model after removing the fluctuations of the vector norms, yielding improved global synchronization guarantees for the homogeneous Kuramoto model. We then recover the latent geometry from the leading eigenspace. When $np\gg\log n$, both the latent vector and relative Gram matrix errors vanish provided $d\ll np\log(1/p)/\log n$. The required lower dimension is only $d\gg\log(1/p)$ for the spherical model and $d\gg\log^2(1/p)\log n$ for the Gaussian model, improving the recovery guarantees of Li and Schramm (2023). Finally, we prove the first exact recovery result for the Gaussian mixture block model of Li and Schramm (2023). At the optimal connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, a polynomial-time semidefinite program exactly recovers all labels in a moderate-separation regime, whereas larger separation makes exact recovery impossible because isolated vertices appear with high probability. Our proofs combine orthogonal polynomial expansions, decoupling, and matrix concentration, avoiding the trace-moment arguments used in previous work.

</details>


### [198] [Optimal Self-Distillation for Rectified Flow via Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14947)
*Saptarshi Roy,Debepsita Mukherjee,Pratik Patil*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于纠正流（RF）的最优自蒸馏（SD）方法，证明了在岭正则化线性RF中，通过最优混合系数（满足符号规则）可以严格提升教师模型的速度场估计质量，并给出了无需网格搜索的单次GCV/验证调参方法，实验验证了该方法在速度风险、模式恢复和有限步生成上的改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型越来越多地使用模型生成的信号进行训练，这既带来了自我改进的机会，也带来了崩溃的风险。本文旨在研究在纠正流框架下，如何通过最优自蒸馏策略，使得基于次优教师速度场训练的学生模型能够可证明地改进教师模型。

Method: 针对线性RF与岭正则化，在固定插值对上证明了精确的仿射路径恒等式，推导了最优混合系数的闭式解，并建立了符号规则（正混合纠正欠正则化教师，负混合纠正过正则化教师）。提出了单次广义交叉验证（GCV）和验证调参方法，避免了网格搜索和重复重拟合。结合RF Wasserstein收敛界，证明了最优自蒸馏能改进控制连续时间和有限步生成误差的速度估计项。在Gaussian模型、Gaussian混合模型和图像数据上进行了实验验证。

Result: 证明了当教师风险沿正则化路径非平稳时，最优自蒸馏能严格改进综合速度风险。最优混合系数满足符号规则。提出的GCV/验证调参方法避免了网格搜索。实验结果表明最优自蒸馏在速度风险、模式恢复和有限步生成方面均优于教师模型和纯蒸馏。

Conclusion: 本文为纠正流中的自蒸馏提供了理论保证和实用算法，证明了最优混合策略可以系统性地改进教师模型，为生成模型的自我改进提供了理论基础和高效调参方案。

Abstract: Modern generative models are increasingly trained using model-generated signals, creating both opportunities for self-improvement and risks of collapse. We study optimal self-distillation (SD) for rectified flow (RF): given a suboptimal teacher velocity field, can a student trained on a mixture of true RF velocities and teacher velocities provably improve the teacher? For linear RF with ridge regularization on fixed interpolation pairs, we prove an exact affine path identity, derive the optimal mixing coefficient in closed form, and show strict improvement in integrated velocity risk whenever the teacher risk is nonstationary along the regularization path. The optimal coefficient obeys a sign rule: positive mixing corrects under-regularized teachers, while negative mixing corrects over-regularized teachers. We also give one-shot generalized cross-validation (GCV) and validation tuning procedure that avoids grid search over mixing weights and repeated refitting. Combining this theorem with RF Wasserstein convergence bounds, we show that optimal self-distillation improves the velocity estimation terms controlling continuous-time and finite-step generation error. Experiments with Gaussian models, Gaussian mixtures, and image data show that optimal self-distillation improves velocity risk, mode recovery, and finite-step generation relative to both the teacher and pure distillation.

</details>


### [199] [cGAP: Generalized Association Plots with HOMALS-Guided Heatmaps for Visualization of High-Dimensional Categorical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15018)
*Chun-houh Chen,Shun-Chuan Chang,Chiun-How Kao,Yi-Ju Lee,Shang-Ying Shiu,Yin-Jing Tien,ShengLi Tzeng,Han-Ming Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出cGAP，一种面向高维分类数据的可视化框架。它通过Homogeneity Analysis将类别数据嵌入三维RGB颜色空间，保留原始数据矩阵的同时增加可解释的几何结构，并整合三种协调视图（HOMALS引导热图、主体邻近矩阵、变量邻近矩阵）以及序列化重排算法，以揭示聚类、异常值和全局-局部结构。在多个真实数据集上展示了其透明探索性分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维分类数据广泛存在，但现有可视化方法要么扩展性差，要么依赖脱离原始数据矩阵的低维显示，要么优先考虑预测精度而非可解释性。cGAP旨在填补这一空白，提供一种既能保存原始矩阵又能赋予可解释几何结构的全矩阵热图可视化框架。

Method: cGAP使用Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS)将主体和类别水平嵌入三维欧几里得空间，将嵌入映射到RGB颜色坐标，使相似模式呈现相似颜色。框架整合三种协调视图：HOMALS引导的原始数据矩阵热图、主体邻近矩阵和变量邻近矩阵，并通过序列化算法重排行列以揭示连贯簇、异常值和局部到全局结构。同时推导了重心可追溯性、投影畸变和对比保持性质。

Result: 通过学生-动物分类数据、哺乳动物齿列数据、UCI蘑菇数据集和COG基因数据库等多个应用案例展示了cGAP的实用性，结果表明cGAP能够有效维持衍生视觉结构与原始分类观测之间的可追溯性，支持透明的探索性分析。

Conclusion: cGAP提供了一个全矩阵、基于热图的可视化环境，适用于跨科学领域的复杂分类数据集分析，在保留原始数据完整性的同时增强了可解释性和可探索性。

Abstract: High-dimensional categorical data arise in genetics, biomedicine, and the social sciences, yet visualization tools for such data remain far less developed than those for continuous variables. Existing methods either scale poorly, rely heavily on low-dimensional displays detached from the original data matrix, or prioritize predictive accuracy over interpretability. To address this gap, we introduce categorical Generalized Association Plots (cGAP), a visualization framework for nominal, ordinal, and binary data that preserves the original data matrix while augmenting it with interpretable geometric structure. cGAP uses Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS) to embed subjects and category levels in a three-dimensional Euclidean space and maps the embedding to red-green-blue coordinates so that similar patterns receive similar colors. The framework integrates three coordinated views: a HOMALS-guided heatmap of the raw data matrix, a subject proximity matrix, and a variable proximity matrix. Seriation algorithms are then used to reorder rows and columns to reveal coherent clusters, outliers, and local-to-global structure. We also derive barycentric traceability, projection-distortion, and contrast-preservation properties that clarify how embedding geometry is transferred to the display. We demonstrate the versatility of cGAP through applications to student-animal classification data, mammalian dentition profiles, mushroom records from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, and the Clusters of Orthologous Genes database. These examples show that cGAP supports transparent exploratory analysis by maintaining traceability between derived visual structure and the original categorical observations. cGAP provides a full-matrix, heatmap-based visualization environment for investigating complex categorical datasets across scientific domains.

</details>


### [200] [Subjective Risk Decomposition: A New View for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15196)
*Raghad Alamri,Michele Caprio,Gavin Brown*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定性量化并非原始概念，而是高层建模决策的产物。通过基于严格适当损失的主观风险分解，可推导出认知不确定性和偶然不确定性度量。反向交叉熵分解恢复了经典信息论不确定性项，并为UQ文献中的众多度量提供了统一理论基础。从学习理论角度，作者引入了主观风险对应的超额风险、逼近误差和估计误差，建立了UQ与学习理论的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化研究往往将不确定性度量视为需要公理和论证的原始概念，缺乏统一的理论基础。作者试图改变这一视角，将不确定性度量视为高层建模决策的后果，而非原始概念，从而为UQ领域提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 通过基于严格适当损失的主观风险分解，将认知不确定性和偶然不确定性度量从高层建模决策中推导出来。以反向交叉熵为例展示分解过程，并证明该方法能恢复UQ文献中已有的众多度量。进一步将视角扩展到学习理论，引入主观风险对应的超额风险、逼近误差和估计误差概念。

Result: 证明了严格适当损失下的主观风险分解可以系统性地导出认知和偶然不确定性度量；反向交叉熵分解恢复经典信息论不确定性项；该方法为UQ领域已有的大量度量提供了共同理论基础；建立了UQ与学习理论中超额风险、逼近误差和估计误差之间的关联。

Conclusion: 不确定性度量是高层建模决策的后果而非原始概念，基于严格适当损失的主观风险分解为UQ提供了统一的理论基础。该工作可作为建立完整UQ学习理论框架的第一步。

Abstract: We present a novel viewpoint for uncertainty quantification. Uncertainty measures are not primitives, in need of axioms and argumentation, but instead consequences, of higher-level modelling decisions. We show how epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty measures can be derived via decomposition of a subjective risk, based on a strictly proper loss. Reverse cross-entropy provides a prominent example, where decomposition recovers the classic information-theoretic uncertainty terms. The same approach recovers numerous measures previously proposed across the UQ literature, providing them a common theoretical foundation. From a practical point of view, this suggests a new approach to UQ: given a modelling scenario and strictly proper loss, the corresponding epistemic and aleatoric terms are induced by the subjective-risk decomposition. We then extend our view to learning theory: we introduce and analyse subjective risk analogues of excess risk, approximation error, and estimation error, and identify the connections to UQ. We consider this a first step towards a full learning-theoretic framework for uncertainty quantification.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Precise sample covariance spectral norm error -- an RDT view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14460)
*Mihailo Stojnic*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文基于随机对偶理论（RDT）框架，超越传统尺度刻画，精确确定了中心高斯分布样本协方差误差谱范数的极限值，并通过上下界匹配在渐近大维度下达成闭合解，数值实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究[66]虽然揭示了样本协方差误差的正确缩放阶次以及有效秩和真实协方差谱的关键作用，但仅停留在误差量级的尺度刻画层面，未能给出误差谱范数的精确极限值。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供精确的闭式极限结果。

Method: 提出基于随机对偶理论（RDT）的通用框架：首先推导RDT上界的闭合显式表达式；然后引入新的双线性-二次型RDT下界机制，结合双副本系统（two-replica systems）的边界策略，证明在大维度条件下上下界匹配，从而得到精确的极限值。

Result: 在渐近大维度下，RDT上下界严格匹配，给出了样本协方差误差谱范数的精确极限值；数值模拟表明，即使维度问题规模在千数量级时，理论结果与实际误差已达到极好的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文通过RDT框架成功将样本协方差误差的研究从尺度刻画推进到精确极限刻画，所提下界机制和双副本策略具有通用性，可推广至其他高维统计推断问题。

Abstract: We study the sample covariance error of centered Gaussians. A remarkable breakthrough [66] established the correct error scaling order and explicitly revealed the critical role of both the effective rank and the true covariance spectrum.
  In this work, we move beyond scaling characterizations and determine the precise limiting value of the error's spectral norm. To do so, we develop a generic framework based on Random Duality Theory (RDT). Within this framework, we first determine closed-form, explicit RDT-based upper bounds. We then establish complementary lower bounds by introducing a novel bilinear-quadratic RDT lower-bounding mechanism. By combining this mechanism with a two-replica systems bounding strategy, we show that our lower and upper bounds match in large-dimensional contexts. Our theoretical results are supplemented with numerical evaluations and simulations, demonstrating an excellent agreement already for problem sizes on the order of thousands.

</details>


### [202] [No Universal Multiplicative FDR Bound for the Benjamini-Hochberg Procedure with Correlated Two-Sided Gaussian Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14812)
*Lihua Lei*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明Benjamini-Hochberg程序在双侧高斯p值且相关矩阵无限制时，其FDR膨胀因子FDR(BH_q)/q在q→0时会发散，下限为Ω(q√log(1/q))；并在共同因子高斯模型下证明了匹配的上界O(q√log(1/q))，表明该下界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战一个广为流传的猜想：即使BH过程在相关高斯p值下不能精确控制FDR，它仍能以通用乘法常数控制FDR。作者希望严格证明该猜想是错误的，并刻画FDR膨胀的确切速度。

Method: 构造特定的高斯模型（相关矩阵无限制），分析BH过程在q→0时最坏情况下的FDR；同时针对共同因子高斯模型（任意均值和载荷）推导匹配的上界。

Result: 证明了FDR(BH_q)/q在q→0时发散，下界至少为cq√log(1/q)；在共同因子高斯模型下得到了相同阶的上界O(q√log(1/q))，表明下界在该类模型中是最优的。

Conclusion: BH过程在相关高斯p值下的最坏情况FDR膨胀速度可达到q√log(1/q)，且该速度在共同因子模型下是紧的，推翻了FDR被通用乘法常数控制的猜想。

Abstract: We study the worst-case false discovery rate of the Benjamini-Hochberg procedure applied to two-sided Gaussian p-values when the correlation matrix is otherwise unrestricted. Dobriban [2026] shows that BH does not always control the FDR at its nominal level. An analogous folklore conjecture is that BH controls the FDR up to a universal multiplicative constant. We prove that this conjecture is false. In particular, we construct Gaussian models for which the inflation factor FDR(BH_q)/q diverges as q tends to 0. More precisely, for all sufficiently small q, the supremum over the number of hypotheses, mean vector, and correlation matrix is at least cq\sqrt{log(1/q)} for a universal constant c > 0. Finally, for a broad class of common-factor Gaussian models with arbitrary means and loadings, we prove the matching-order upper bound FDR(BH_q) = O(q\sqrt{log(1/q)}), and hence the lower bound is sharp in order for this class.

</details>


### [203] [Measuring Spatial Clustering via Metropolis-Hastings Diffusion Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14880)
*Thomas Weighill,Chidinma Williams*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的图概率分布差异度量——扩散距离（Diffusion Distance），通过图约束马尔可夫链的收敛速率衡量分布差异，并展示了其作为空间聚类统计检验工具的有效性，在合成数据和实际城市种族隔离数据分析中均优于传统Moran's I指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间自相关度量（如Moran's I）主要捕捉局部空间模式，无法充分利用全局图几何信息。作者希望提出一种能够整合全局图结构、更精确刻画分布差异的度量方法，并构建相应的统计检验框架。

Method: 定义扩散距离为两个概率分布在图约束马尔可夫链（以Metropolis-Hastings转移矩阵为默认选择）下的收敛速率；建立与图谱和最优传输的理论联系；推导置换零假设下的高概率界和精确谱公式，实现高效统计检验。

Result: 理论层面建立了与图谱和最优传输的联系，证明了稳定性结果；实证方面，在随机块模型合成数据上扩散距离比Moran's I具有更高统计功效；对美国100个城市的黑人人口分布分析显示，扩散距离能检测到Moran's I无法识别的细微城市隔离模式差异。

Conclusion: 扩散距离作为一种基于图全局几何的分布差异度量，在理论和应用上均优于仅依赖局部模式的Moran's I，为空间聚类检测和城市隔离分析提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: We propose a novel measure of the discrepancy between two probability distributions $f$ and $g$ on a graph - which we call the diffusion distance - that measures the rate of convergence of $f$ to $g$ under a graph-constrained Markov chain with stationary distribution $g$. As a default choice for this Markov chain, we use the Metropolis-Hastings transition matrix targeting $g$ with proposals given by a random walk on the graph. Our primary case of interest is when the second distribution $g$ is uniform, in which case the diffusion distance becomes a measure of spatial clustering in $f$. Used in this way, (Metropolis-Hastings) diffusion distance to uniformity extends Moran's $I$-type measures of spatial autocorrelation by incorporating global graph geometry rather than just local patterns. Indeed, Moran's $I$, the most well-known measure of spatial autocorrelation, can be viewed as a one-step heuristic for diffusion distance, so long as specific spatial weights are used. We establish theoretical bounds and a stability result for our measure, connecting it to graph spectra and optimal transport. We then turn our attention to outlining a statistical test for spatial clustering using diffusion distance. Under permutation null models, we derive high-probability bounds on diffusion distance underpinned by exact spectral formulas for convergence of distributions, enabling an efficient statistical test for spatial clustering on large datasets. We empirically compare diffusion distance to Moran's $I$ both as a numerical measure and as a statistical test. We show that diffusion distance exhibits higher power on synthetic data using a stochastic block model. Empirical analysis of Black population distributions for 100 U.S. cities shows that diffusion distance detects subtle differences in urban segregation patterns that Moran's $I$ does not.

</details>


### [204] [Testing for correct model specification in copula regression models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14930)
*Holger Dette,Philip Dörr*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对半参数Copula回归模型提出了一种基于加权L2距离的拟合优度检验方法，通过核估计量评估回归函数的设定偏差，并构建了经典检验和自归一化序列统计量的相关检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半参数Copula回归模型依赖于参数化Copula族的正确设定，但现有方法缺乏直接针对回归函数层面的模型误设定检验，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 定义真实回归函数与Copula回归模型最佳逼近之间的加权L2距离，提出其核估计量，证明该估计量在正确设定和固定备择下均具有相合性和渐近正态性；基于此构建经典设定检验和自归一化序列统计量的枢轴置信区间与检验。

Result: 核估计量具有相合性和渐近正态性；经典检验和自归一化检验均有效；有限样本模拟显示准确的水平逼近和良好的检验功效。

Conclusion: 所提出的检验方法能有效评估Copula回归模型的回归函数层面的误设定，具有良好的理论性质和有限样本表现。

Abstract: We propose a goodness-of-fit test for semiparametric copula regression models. Such models express the regression function in terms of marginal distribution functions and copula densities and therefore provide a flexible way to avoid fully nonparametric estimation in high-dimensional regression problems. Their performance, however, depends crucially on the specification of the parametric copula family. Instead of testing the copula model itself, we assess misspecification directly at the level of the induced regression function. To this end, we introduce a weighted $L^2$-distance between the true regression function and its best approximation within the postulated copula regression model. A kernel-based estimator of this distance is proposed and shown to be consistent and asymptotically normal under both the null hypothesis of correct specification and fixed alternatives. We derive a classical specification test and, using a self-normalized sequential statistic, construct pivotal confidence intervals and tests for relevant deviations from the model. Finite-sample simulations demonstrate accurate level approximation and good power properties of the proposed procedures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [PiVoT: A Variational Solution for Real-time Large-scale Multi-object Detection and Tracking under Heavy Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13891)
*Runze Gan,Qing Li,Simon J. Godsill,Mike E. Davies,James R. Hopgood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PiVoT是一种快速、抗杂乱的贝叶斯多目标跟踪器，通过对位置和多普勒测量进行端到端联合推断（包括状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联和测量率），在无需外部聚类或检测器、无需训练的条件下，在严重杂波、大规模目标及全分辨率多普勒点云场景中显著优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器，并实现了与深度学习检测基准相当的性能和实时运行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于泊松测量模型的贝叶斯跟踪器在严重杂波、大目标群体和全分辨率多普勒点云下精度和效率不足，且许多雷达应用数据稀缺，难以依赖深度学习方案。

Method: 提出PiVoT，一种基于变分推理的端到端多目标跟踪器，创新包括：理论支持的出生剪枝、二次到线性复杂度约简以实现精确更新、计算高效的多普勒泊松模型，联合推断目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联和测量率。

Result: PiVoT在挑战性场景中显著优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器；可扩展到上千个目标；对视觉上与目标不可分的杂波具有鲁棒性；在完整现代汽车雷达数据集上实时运行，性能可与深度学习检测基准相媲美。

Conclusion: PiVoT作为无需训练的联合检测与跟踪器，在数据稀缺的雷达应用中实现了准确性、鲁棒性和效率的综合突破，为贝叶斯方法在复杂雷达场景中的实用化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-object detection and tracking from noisy point clouds remain challenging in many data-scarce radar applications. Current Bayesian trackers based on Poisson measurement models offer a training-free solution but struggle to achieve accuracy and efficiency under severe clutter, large object populations, and full-resolution Doppler point clouds. We address this with PiVoT, a fast, clutter-resilient multi-object tracker for both positional and Doppler measurements. PiVoT performs end-to-end detection and tracking of a large and time-varying number of objects without external clustering or detectors, through joint inference of object states, shapes, existence probabilities, data association, and measurement rates. Its efficiency is driven by several variational inference innovations, such as theoretically justified birth pruning, quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions for exact updates, and a computationally efficient Doppler Poisson model. Experiments show that PiVoT substantially outperforms existing Bayesian trackers in challenging scenes, while also demonstrating exceptional scalability to a thousand objects, robustness to clutter visually inseparable from objects, and real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets, where it attains performance comparable to a deep-learning detection benchmark as a training-free joint detector and tracker.

</details>


### [206] [A Temporal Machine Learning-Based Time-to-Event Model for Predicting ALS Progression and Healthcare Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14190)
*Zongliang Yue,Qi Li,Terry Heiman-Patterson,Frank Bearoff,Zhaohui Qin,Huanmei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数字孪生启发的生存分析框架，整合纵向ALSFRS-R轨迹与生存建模，实现肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）患者功能衰退与辅助设备使用的个性化预测，并通过Cox比例风险模型和时序机器学习模型识别出下肢功能（尤其是行走和爬楼梯）是轮椅使用的最强预测因子，最终生成个性化的无轮椅生存曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS是一种进展性和异质性极强的神经退行性疾病，临床上难以准确预测如辅助设备使用等关键里程碑事件的发生时间，现有方法缺乏个性化和动态预测能力。

Method: 构建包含诊断记录、ALSFRS-R评估、日常生活活动和人口统计信息的纵向数据集；基于相关聚类识别功能域；使用广义加性混合模型刻画非线性功能衰退；开发时序机器学习模型预测纵向衰退；采用Cox比例风险模型识别轮椅使用的关键预测因子；最终实现数字孪生启发的时序机器学习生存预测模型，生成个性化生存曲线。

Result: 下肢功能（尤其是行走和爬楼梯）是轮椅使用的最强预测因子；所提出的数字孪生框架能够动态生成个性化的无轮椅生存曲线，实现阶段依赖的疾病进展预测。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可扩展、可解释且临床可操作的工具，将ALS进展与个性化决策支持相连接，适用于主动护理规划、临床试验分层和精准医学应用。

Abstract: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) is a progressive and heterogeneous neurodegenerative disease in which predicting clinically meaningful milestones, such as assistive device use, remains challenging. We developed a time-to-event, digital-twin-inspired framework that integrates longitudinal ALS Functional Rating Scale-Revised (ALSFRS-R) trajectories with survival modeling to support individualized prediction of functional decline and assistive device utilization. We constructed a harmonized longitudinal dataset by integrating diagnosis records, ALSFRS-R assessments, activities of daily living, and demographic information, followed by preprocessing to ensure data quality, temporal alignment, and cohort consistency. Correlation-based clustering identified coherent functional domains spanning bulbar, upper limb, axial, lower limb, and respiratory systems. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear, domain-specific functional decline across all domains. In addition, a temporal machine learning model was developed to predict longitudinal functional decline and capture stage-dependent disease progression. Cox proportional hazards modeling further identified lower limb function, particularly walking and stair climbing, as the strongest predictors of earlier wheelchair access. Building on these results, we implemented a digital twin-inspired temporal machine learning-based time-to-event (TTE) model that generates individualized survival curves and dynamically predicts wheelchair-free survival. This framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and clinically actionable approach for linking ALS progression with personalized decision support, with applications in proactive care planning, clinical trial stratification, and precision medicine.

</details>


### [207] [Supervised Fine-Tuning vs. In-Context Learning: An Equilibrium Analysis of LLM Personalization under Congestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14371)
*Fengzhuo Zhang,Zhuoran Yang,Dirk Bergemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型服务中个性化算法（监督微调SFT vs. 上下文学习ICL）的选择困境，分析了用户间的计算资源竞争如何影响激励，并提出了平台设计策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化虽能提升LLM性能，但消耗稀缺计算资源，用户需共享。核心矛盾在于：用户何时应选择昂贵的SFT而非轻量的ICL？其他用户的个性化决策造成的拥塞如何改变激励？平台提供多种个性化算法时应采取何种策略？

Method: 构建了一个可处理的LLM服务框架，捕捉用户面临的统计-经济学权衡。通过理论分析推导不同算法主导的机制，研究均衡资源消耗的非单调性，并证明双算法策略对平台利润的影响。

Result: 1) ICL和SFT在不同机制下各自占优，由预训练覆盖率和数据信噪比决定，但拥塞可翻转排序；2) 均衡资源消耗呈现显著非单调性：预训练精度提高降低拥塞，但更广的预训练覆盖率和更困难的任务有时会增加拥塞；3) 提供两种个性化方法从不损害平台最大利润，尽管可能增加计算负载。实验验证理论预测，平台调查显示同时提供SFT和ICL的比例从2021年9.5%升至2025年71.4%。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM服务中个性化算法选择的复杂经济权衡，为平台设计提供了理论指导：双算法策略在利润上占优，且与行业实践趋势高度一致。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI services, but a critical tension emerges: while personalization improves model performance, it consumes scarce computational resources that users must share. When should a user invest in expensive Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) versus lightweight In-Context Learning (ICL)? How does congestion from other users' personalization choices reshape these incentives? And what strategies should platforms adopt when offering multiple personalization algorithms?
  We develop a tractable framework for LLM serving that captures the statistical-economic trade-offs users face. Our analysis yields several surprising insights. First, we show that ICL and SFT dominate in different regimes, determined by an interplay between pretraining coverage and data signal-to-noise ratios, but congestion can flip these rankings. Second, equilibrium resource consumption exhibits pronounced non-monotonicity: improving pretraining precision reduces the congestion, while broader pretraining coverage and harder tasks sometimes increase it. Third, we prove that offering both personalization methods never hurts the platform's maximal profits, despite potentially increasing computational load.
  Experiments with GPT-2 on linear regression tasks validate our theoretical predictions about algorithm performance. Complementing these results, our review of documentation from 21 major AI platforms shows that the share offering both SFT and ICL increased from 9.5% in 2021 to 71.4% in 2025, consistent with our platform-design implications.

</details>


### [208] [Adaptive Runge-Kutta Step Control Buys Training Loss, Not Generalization: An Honest Compute-Matched Study of RK-Adam Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14516)
*Akhilesh Gogikar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将优化器解释为梯度流离散化促使人们将高阶龙格-库塔（RK）积分器应用于神经网络。本文在严格的计算匹配协议下构建了代表性Adam变体（Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对、FSAL复用、局部误差步长控制），发现RK变体在小批量和全批量训练中均逊于普通Adam。分析表明其"自适应性"是虚假的：归一化误差远低于容差，步长从第一步起就锁定在增长上限。修复该方法的真正拒绝分支后，在全批量训练中比调优后的Adam低约40倍训练损失，但收益对初始步长敏感且无法提升测试准确率。预注册的后续实验排除了过拟合和温度调节等解释，最终结论是：高阶自适应积分能实现更深的确定性最小化和小幅正则化效应，但不如更便宜、调优良好的一阶基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受将优化器视为梯度流离散化的启发，研究者希望将高阶龙格-库塔积分器应用于神经网络优化，以期望获得比传统Adam更好的性能。核心动机是探索自适应步长控制（局部误差控制）和梯度平均（级内子步）是否能带来实质性的优化收益。

Method: 构建了基于Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对的Adam变体，包含FSAL（首步同末步复用）和局部误差步长控制。在严格的计算匹配协议下（相同梯度评估预算）进行小批量和全批量训练评估。通过仪器化分析自适应行为，并设计了修复版本（真拒绝分支、在应用映射上计算误差）进行对比实验。后续进行预注册实验排除了过拟合和温度调节等替代解释。

Result: 在计算匹配协议下，RK变体在训练损失上输给普通Adam。自适应步长控制是虚假的：归一化误差远低于容差，98-100%的步长被锁定在增长上限，容差跨越100倍仍产生位一致轨迹。实际上是固定步长Adam加上3-4倍成本的梯度平均。修复后的版本在全批量训练中比调优Adam低约40倍训练损失，但收益对初始步长敏感，无法提升测试准确率。梯度平均是有效的隐式正则化器（10/10种子优于lr匹配的Adam/AdamW），但RMSprop和NAdam以三分之一成本匹配或超过它。

Conclusion: 高阶自适应积分能实现更深的确定性最小化和小幅正则化效应，但被一个更便宜、调优良好的一阶基线（如RMSprop、NAdam）完全覆盖。轨迹效应（控制器选择的最小值）在测试准确率上比等深度的一阶下降低1.3-3.4个百分点。本质上，高阶RK方法没有提供一阶方法无法提供的实质性优势。

Abstract: Interpreting optimizers as gradient-flow discretizations has motivated applying higher-order Runge-Kutta (RK) integrators to neural networks. We build a representative Adam variant (Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK pair, FSAL reuse, local-error step control) and evaluate it under a strict compute-matched protocol giving every method the same gradient-evaluation budget - an accounting this literature rarely enforces. Under it the RK variant loses to plain Adam on training loss in both minibatch and full-batch (RK's best-case) training. Instrumenting it shows the "adaptivity" is illusory: normalized error stays far below tolerance, the step size pins at its growth cap from step one (98-100 percent of steps), and no rtol x hmax x h0 setting makes it act; tolerances spanning 100x give bit-identical trajectories. The method is exactly fixed-step Adam with an averaged gradient at 3-4x cost. Repairing it (true reject branch; error on the applied map) reverses the full-batch result - about 40x lower training loss than tuned Adam - and a fixed-step control isolates adaptivity (an emergent warmup-and-growth schedule) as the mechanism. But the gain is fragile to the initial step size and does not reach test accuracy. A pre-registered follow-up rules out the obvious explanations: deeper minimization does not overfit, and an explicit temperature knob only hurts - leaving a trajectory effect, the controller selecting a minimum generalizing 1.3-3.4 points below first-order descent at equal depth. An n=10 study confirms one secondary effect: gradient averaging is a genuine implicit regularizer, beating lr-matched Adam and AdamW on 10/10 seeds - yet RMSprop and NAdam match or beat it at a third the per-step cost. Higher-order adaptive integration buys deeper deterministic minimization and a small regularization effect, but nothing a cheaper, well-tuned first-order baseline does not already provide.

</details>


### [209] [Probabilistic Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Estimating Heterogeneous Elastic Properties from Low-Resolution and Noisy Displacement Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14563)
*Tatthapong Srikitrungruang,Jaesung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种概率逆弹性物理信息神经网络（PIE-PINN）框架，用于从噪声大、分辨率低的位移数据中鲁棒地估计杨氏模量和泊松比，通过结合B样条引导的位移网络和分层半柯西模型，实现了对异质性弹性参数的自适应稳健反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低分辨率位移测量下的异质弹性参数估计是严重不适定的逆弹性问题，现有方法依赖高保真观测和人工预设损失权重，适应性差且对噪声和分辨率退化敏感。

Method: PIE-PINN框架：1) 将位移观测、应变-位移关系和平衡残差建模为拉普拉斯分布的统一概率模型；2) 采用B样条引导的位移网络（B样条提供全局平滑表示，神经网络校正局部变化）；3) 引入分层半柯西模型自适应降低严重位移拟合误差的权重；4) 交替最大似然训练策略，通过加权残差最小化更新均值，同时更新尺度以调整损失权重。

Result: 在不同噪声水平和观测分辨率下的系统案例研究表明，PIE-PINN框架具有良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PIE-PINN通过概率建模和自适应加权机制，有效应对了低分辨率、高噪声条件下的逆弹性问题，提升了估计的鲁棒性和适应性。

Abstract: Estimating spatially heterogeneous elastic properties from low-resolution displacement measurements is a severely ill-posed inverse elasticity problem because low resolution obscures spatial details needed to distinguish heterogeneous property variations, and small measurement perturbations or fitting errors are amplified through inverse estimation. Existing inverse methods often rely on high-fidelity observations and manually prespecified loss weights, limiting their adaptability and making them sensitive to noise and resolution degradation. We propose a Probabilistic Inverse Elasticity Physics-Informed Neural Network (PIE-PINN) framework for robust estimation of Young's modulus and Poisson's ratio from noisy, low-resolution displacement data. PIE-PINN models displacement observation, strain-discrepancy, and equilibrium residuals using Laplace distributions within a unified probabilistic model. To improve robustness, the framework combines a B-spline-guided displacement network with a hierarchical half-Cauchy model for displacement residual scales. The B-spline provides a smooth global representation of the displacement field, while the neural network correction captures local variations. The hierarchical scale model adaptively downweights severe displacement fitting errors, enabling more robust recovery of the latent mean displacement field. An alternating maximum-likelihood training strategy updates the mean through weighted residual minimization and updates the scales to adjust the loss weights. Systematic case studies across varying noise levels and observation resolutions demonstrate the robustness of PIE-PINN.

</details>


### [210] [Sharp Stability Threshold and Certification for Designing Stable Residual Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14576)
*Hyemin Gu,Michael Tyrrell,Tuhin Sahai,Markos A. Katsoulakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出残差架构的亚线性增长原理：残差块的速度场需满足 $\|v(x, t)\| \leq c\|x\|^q + b$ 且 $q \le 1$，这是网络稳定训练的必要充分条件。通过经典ODE理论和HJB最优控制分析双重论证了阈值 $q=1$ 的严格性。基于输入量级指数的运算规则，可在架构原语层面高效验证稳定性。实验在Mamba和PatchTST上验证了该准则的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度残差网络的训练稳定性缺乏严格的理论标准，传统依赖层归一化等启发式手段，未能揭示底层数学原理。本文旨在建立一个精确的数学判据来界定稳定训练的边界条件。

Method: 提出亚线性增长原理，通过经典ODE理论证明 $q \le 1$ 时全局前向流存在、$q > 1$ 时速度场发散；通过HJB最优控制方程证明训练最优解是bang-bang控制，$q > 1$ 时最优解爆炸。进一步建立输入量级指数在五种残差块构建操作下的运算法则，实现架构级稳定性验证。

Result: $q \le 1$ 是稳定训练的必要充分条件；解释了层归一化的稳定化作用；提出无参数修改可将超临界Mamba块从 $q=5$ 降至 $q=1$ 而无需归一化层；Mamba和PatchTST实验证实 $q \le 1$ 变体训练稳定。

Conclusion: 亚线性增长原理为残差架构的稳定性提供了严格的理论基础，$q \le 1$ 判据可替代对归一化层的依赖，指导稳定神经架构的设计。

Abstract: We propose \emph{the sublinear-growth principle} for deep residual architectures -- a sharp stability threshold on the input-magnitude exponent of every residual block's velocity field: $$\|v(x, t)\| \leq c\,\|x\|^q + b, \qquad q \in [0, 1].$$ The threshold $q = 1$ is established via two independent arguments. Classical ODE theory gives a global forward flow on $[0, T]$ at $q \le 1$ and exhibits divergent velocity fields at any $q > 1$. The optimal-control analysis, via the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation, sharpens this to a selection statement: the training optimum is bang-bang on the boundary of the admissible class, so the optimum at $q > 1$ blows up while the optimum at $q \le 1$ is safe by construction. The exponent criterion $q \le 1$ is thereby a necessary and sufficient condition for stable training. It clarifies architectural placements that ensure the stability of training and inference, explaining, for instance, the stabilizing role of layer normalization. The sublinear-growth velocity fields form \emph{the right function space} on which forward dynamics, adjoint sensitivity, and architectural composition are all well-controlled. An arithmetic of input-magnitude exponents under the five operations that build residual blocks enables efficient certification of $q_k \le 1$ at the level of architectural primitives, in place of ad hoc trial and error in the search for stable neural architectural designs. A parameter-free modification reduces the supercritical Mamba block from $q = 5$ to $q = 1$ without layer normalization, demonstrating this point. Experiments on Mamba and PatchTST confirm that the $q \le 1$ variants train stably: the criterion is the input-magnitude exponent, not the presence of a normalization layer.

</details>


### [211] [What's in a Smoothness Constant? Tighter Rates for Local SGD with Bounded Second-order Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14731)
*Kumar Kshitij Patel,Rustem Islamov,Sebastian U Stich,Aurelien Lucchi,Eduard Gorbunov,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明Local SGD在一般凸目标下的收敛性猜想，并给出紧的下界，建立了更精细的收敛理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Local SGD在实践中优于Mini-batch SGD，但现有理论仅在有界二阶异质性假设下对强凸目标有效，其在一般凸目标下的收敛性仍是一个未解决的猜想。

Method: 通过理论推导，在有界二阶异质性假设下证明Local SGD在一般凸目标上的改进收敛上界，同时建立新的下界证明上界的紧性。

Result: 证明了Local SGD在一般凸目标下的收敛性猜想，改进了该设置下已知最优下界，并给出了串行SGD在下采样场景中的下界。

Conclusion: 有界二阶异质性能够统一刻画Local SGD在强凸和一般凸目标下的收敛行为，为理解局部更新在数据异构下的有效性提供了更完整的理论支撑。

Abstract: Local SGD, also known as Federated Averaging, is a widely used distributed optimization algorithm. Although Local SGD often outperforms alternatives such as Mini-batch SGD in practice, theory still only partially explains when and why local updates help under realistic data heterogeneity. Recent work by [Patel et al., 2025] shows that a bounded second-order heterogeneity assumption captures the efficiency of Local SGD for strongly convex objectives, and conjectures that the same principle extends to the general convex setting.
